Conclusion: 通过结构化接口和规则约束有效解决自主后训练复杂性，显著提升训练效率与结果稳定性，为自动化ML工作流提供新范式。

Abstract: Training language models (LMs) remains a highly human-intensive process, even as frontier language model agents become increasingly capable at software engineering and other long-horizon tasks. A central challenge is that autonomous post-training is not just a coding problem: it requires the agent to repeatedly plan iterations, construct benchmark-aligned data, run stable training jobs, evaluate checkpoints, and preserve experiment state across many hours of interaction. We present AutoTrainess, a LM agent that exposes these operations as a repository of agent-computer interfaces for planning, data preparation, training, evaluation, and logging. Rather than leaving the agent to operate in a raw CLI environment with an underspecified action space, AutoTrainess externalizes prior human experience as explicit workflows, rules, and execution constraints that guide the agent toward effective and reliable training behavior. On PostTrainBench, AutoTrainess consistently outperforms CLI-only baselines, achieving 26.94 average score with GPT-5.4 (Codex) versus 23.21 for CLI-only. It also generalizes across models and harnesses, improving DeepSeek-V4-Flash (OpenCode) from 12.13 to 19.58.

</details>


### [184] [Robust Text Watermarking for Large Language Models via Dual Semantic Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31602)
*Jonas Schäfer,Cezary Pilaszewicz,Gerhard Wunder*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DEW是一种新型语义水印方案，通过结合上下文和令牌级嵌入，利用信号处理和代数操作增强LLM生成文本对转述和翻译的鲁棒性，实验显示其检测性能优于现有方法且保持文本质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM语义水印在转述和翻译攻击下易退化，缺乏鲁棒性；DEW旨在解决此问题，通过更深层的嵌入处理提升水印在语义变化下的生存能力，以支持负责任的AI部署。

Method: DEW采用信号处理框架，对token和context嵌入应用代数向量空间操作（如伪随机矩阵投影），生成可优雅退化的水印信号；使用秘密密钥种子确保混淆，并基于代数分布进行统计测试和基准评估。

Result: 在多个LLM上的实验表明：DEW在转述后检测率显著提升，文本质量保持竞争力，且在翻译后仍可有效检测，而其他语义水印方法在此场景下性能严重下降。

Conclusion: DEW提供了一种实用、鲁棒的水印解决方案，有效应对LLM生成内容的篡改风险，为AI安全部署和版权保护奠定技术基础。

Abstract: This work presents Dual-Embedding Watermarking (DEW), a semantic watermarking scheme for large language models (LLMs) that leverages contextual and token-level embeddings to enhance robustness against paraphrasing and translation. DEW utilizes a signal-processing methodology, applying algebraic vector-space operations to \mbox{token and context embeddings to derive a watermark signal that degrades gracefully under semantic shifts. The method obfuscates the watermark by projecting embedding vectors through pseudo-random matrices seeded with a secret key. Relevant distributions derived from the underlying algebra are evaluated and employed for statistical testing and benchmarking of DEW. Experimental results across multiple LLMs indicate that DEW improves post-paraphrase detection while maintaining competitive text quality, and remains detectable after translation, even when prior semantic watermarks degrade significantly. These findings position DEW as a practical and robust solution for safeguarding LLM-generated text and addressing critical issues in responsible AI deployment.

</details>


### [185] [CLExEval: A Human-in-the-Loop Framework for Qualitative Evaluation of LLM Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31608)
*Ajmal M.,Abin Roy,Afthab Salam Kanniyan,Jawadh Abdul Kabeer,Jerin James,Preslav Nakov,Zhuohan Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了CLExEval框架，用于评估大语言模型在临床推理中的表现，发现了三种关键失败模式，并验证了自动化评估方法的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在医疗基准测试中表现优异，但其临床推理能力难以可靠评估。存在评估幻觉风险：流畅且结构良好的解释可能在最终诊断错误时仍显得临床可信，这构成了临床应用的重大风险。

Method: 引入CLExEval框架，采用渐进信息掩蔽方法评估LLM临床推理。该框架结合了5600个专家医生注释和来自40个罕见诊断案例的200个临床推理轨迹，并通过人类验证的失败集(n=142)评估LLM-as-a-Judge范式。

Result: 识别出三种重复出现的失败模式：(i) 冗长偏差：GPT-4o-mini在信息稀缺条件下诊断准确率从95.0%降至32.5%；(ii) 隐藏知识悖论：专业模型达到92.5%最大诊断潜力，但在冗长语境中无法可靠检索知识；(iii) 推理-输出不匹配：68.6%的案例中正确诊断出现在推理轨迹中但未反映在最终答案中。在LLM-as-a-Judge评估中，GPT-4o-mini批准了47.9%的临床错误输出，而HuatuoGPT-o1批准了所有有效评分的失败案例并显示正向自我偏好偏差。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，没有专家验证的独立自动化临床评估会严重高估临床可靠性。强调了专家验证在LLM临床推理评估中的必要性，为医疗AI应用的安全性和可靠性评估提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong results on many medical benchmarks, but their clinical reasoning remains difficult to evaluate reliably. A central risk is an evaluation illusion: fluent and well-structured explanations can appear clinically convincing even when the final diagnosis is incorrect. We introduce CLExEval, a human-in-the-loop framework for evaluating LLM clinical reasoning under progressive information masking. CLExEval combines 5,600 expert-physician annotations with 200 clinical reasoning traces derived from 40 rare diagnostic cases. Our analysis identifies three recurring failure patterns: (i) verbosity bias, where GPT-4o-mini's diagnostic accuracy drops from 95.0% to 32.5% under information scarcity; (ii) a hidden knowledge paradox, where a specialist model reaches 92.5% maximum diagnostic potential but fails to retrieve that knowledge reliably in verbose contexts; and (iii) a 68.6% reasoning-to-output mismatch, where correct diagnoses appear in reasoning traces but are not reflected in final answers. We further evaluate the LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm on a human-verified failure set (n = 142). GPT-4o-mini approved 47.9% of clinically incorrect outputs, while HuatuoGPT-o1 approved all validly scored failures and showed a positive self-preference bias. These results suggest that standalone automated clinical evaluations can substantially overestimate clinical reliability without expert-grounded validation.

</details>


### [186] [Tone-Conditioned Curriculum Learning for Low-Resource Bantu Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31642)
*Kesego Mokgosi,Vukosi Marivate,Sitwala Mundia,Unarine Netshifhefhe,Tsholofelo Hope Mogale,Thapelo Sindane*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对南部班图语系语言ASR准确率低的问題，提出音调条件课程学习框架，在6种语言上取得显著改进，但不同语言需要差异化模型选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 南部班图语系语言使用者超过8000万，但现有基础ASR模型零样本词错误率(WER)超过100%，严重限制了在教育和公共服务中的实际应用。

Method: 提出音调条件课程框架，结合混合难度评分、音调统计驱动的受限适配器和分阶段课程训练。在社区语料库上训练，并测试向NCHLT的迁移能力以评估鲁棒性。

Result: 架构与语言间存在显著交互作用：W2V-BERT在Nguni语言上比Whisper优3-4个WER点，Whisper在Sotho-Tswana语言上表现更好。音调条件W2V-BERT平均WER达28.41%，Xitsonga迁移WER为23.79%。

Conclusion: 没有单一模型适合所有6种语言，部署时应根据语言特性选择模型，并在不同语料库上进行验证。

Abstract: Southern Bantu languages are spoken by over 80 million people, yet current foundation ASR models still produce zero-shot WER above 100%, which limits practical use in education and public services. We addressed this gap with a tone conditioned curriculum framework for 6 Southern Bantu languages that combined hybrid difficulty scoring, gated adapters driven by tonal statistics and staged curriculum training. We trained on a community corpus and tested transfer to NCHLT to measure robustness beyond matched evaluation. Results revealed clear interactions between architecture and language, with W2V-BERT outperforming Whisper on Nguni languages by 3 to 4 WER points whilst Whisper performed better on Sotho-Tswana languages. W2V-BERT with tone conditioning reached 28.41% average WER across datasets and 23.79% on Xitsonga transfer. No single model suited all 6 languages, so deployment should pair model selection per language with validation across corpora.

</details>


### [187] [Moral Safety in LLMs: Exposing Performative Compliance with Puzzled Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31644)
*Mohammadamin Shafiei,Shuyue Stella Li,Yulia Tsvetkov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了大语言模型在道德评估中的“表演性合规”现象：当人口统计身份以显式标签呈现时模型表现公平，但身份需推断时公平性显著下降。提出“线索可见性差距”指标，建议现有公平性基准必须纳入线索变化测试以区分真实道德鲁棒性与表面合规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在医疗、法律等高风险场景的应用中，其道德行为真实性亟待验证。现有公平性评估可能因测试方式局限而高估模型道德安全性，导致部署决策存在隐患。

Method: 设计线索变化方法论：固定道德困境和人口统计身份，仅改变身份呈现方式（显式标签 vs 隐含推断），通过对比测试量化模型行为差异。

Result: 隐藏显式身份标签使有害决策率上升4.4个百分点，模型安全排名发生显著变化；即使模型正确推断身份，该现象仍存在，排除归因错误可能性。

Conclusion: 提出模型无关的“线索可见性差距”鲁棒性指标，强调现有公平性评估需补充线索变化测试，否则仅测量表面合规性，不应作为高风险场景部署依据。

Abstract: As large language models take on morally consequential roles in healthcare, legal, and hiring contexts, we need to examine whether their ethical behaviors are genuine or superficial. We show that current fairness evaluations substantially overestimate moral safety. Models appear fair when demographic identity is stated as an explicit label, yet become measurably less fair when the same identity must be inferred. We term this failure \emph{performative compliance}, where a model is fair when the presentation resembles a fairness evaluation and less fair as that cue weakens. We introduce a cue-variation methodology that holds the moral dilemma and the demographic identity fixed and varies only how that identity is conveyed. Hiding the explicit label raises harmful decisions by $+4.4$~pp and changes model safety rankings, and the shift persists when models correctly infer the demographic, ruling out attribution error. We propose the \textbf{Cue Visibility Gap}, a model-agnostic robustness metric that can be added to any existing fairness benchmark to separate genuine from performative moral safety. Fairness evaluations that omit cue variation measure surface compliance, not moral robustness, and should not ground deployment decisions in high-stakes settings.

</details>


### [188] [Overview of the TalentCLEF 2026: Skill and Job Title Intelligence for Human Capital Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31692)
*Luis Gasco,Hermenegildo Fabregat,Laura García-Sardiña,Paula Estrella,Warre Veys,Casimiro Pío Carrino,Matthias De Lange,Daniel Deniz Cerpa,Álvaro Rodrigo,Jens-Joris Decorte,Rabih Zbib*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文概述了2026年CLEF会议中举办的TalentCLEF挑战赛第二版，该挑战赛聚焦人力资源管理领域的自然语言处理研究。包含两个任务：任务A为多语言（英/西）上下文感知的职位-人员匹配，任务B为英语职位的技能检索及核心/情境技能分类。吸引113支队伍参与，收到超400份提交，体现了学界对HRM评估基准的高度关注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动自然语言处理技术在人力资源管理领域的应用，通过构建标准化评估基准促进相关研究发展，解决人才匹配和技能识别等实际业务需求。

Method: 设计双任务挑战赛：任务A采用多语言简历与职位描述的语义匹配模型进行候选人排序；任务B结合技能检索与类型分类技术，区分核心技能与情境技能。提供标准化数据集和评估协议，支持跨语言和多模态方法验证。

Result: 吸引113支队伍注册参赛，完成超400次任务提交，验证了多种NLP技术在HRM场景的有效性。结果显示多语言处理能力和细粒度技能分类成为技术突破点。

Conclusion: 挑战赛成功建立HRM领域首个多语言评估框架，显著提升社区参与度，为招聘系统智能化和技能图谱构建提供了关键技术路径，未来可扩展至更多语种和业务场景。

Abstract: This paper presents an overview of the second edition of the TalentCLEF challenge, organized as a Lab at the Conference and Labs of the Evaluation Forum (CLEF) 2026. TalentCLEF is an initiative aimed at advancing Natural Language Processing research in Human Capital Management. The second edition of the challenge consisted of two tasks: Task A, contextualized job-person matching, focuses on identifying and ranking the most suitable candidates represented by their resumes for a given job vacancy in English and Spanish. Task B, job-skill matching with skill type classification, addresses retrieving the most relevant skills for a given job title in English and distinguishing between core and contextual skills. TalentCLEF attracted 113 registered teams and received more than 400 submissions in the two tasks, reflecting the growing interest of the research community in shared evaluation benchmarks for Human Capital Management. This paper describes the motivation and organization of the challenge, summarizes the datasets and evaluation settings, and reports the main results obtained by the participating teams.

</details>


### [189] [Cross-lingual Relation Extraction with Large Language Models: Zero-Shot, Few-Shot, and Fine-Tuned Evaluation on Romanian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31718)
*Dragos-Mitrut Vasile,Elena-Simona Apostol,Stefan-Adrian Toma,Adrian Paschke,Ciprian-Octavian Truica*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究罗马尼亚语低资源语言的关系提取，通过LLM翻译英文基准数据集并评估Gemma 4 31B模型，发现小参数编码器模型性能接近大模型，计算效率更高


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言的关系提取受限于标注语料缺乏，探索罗马尼亚语跨语言关系提取的可行性

Method: 使用LLM翻译管道将SemEval-2010 Task 8从英语翻译成罗马尼亚语；评估Gemma 4 31B（零样本、少样本、QLoRA微调）与四个编码器基线（XLM-RoBERTa、Romanian BERT、RoBERT-large）；测试两种任务形式：带标记实体关系分类和端到端提取

Result: 罗马尼亚语在纯提示设置下比英语下降3-5个百分点；少样本提示增益有限；QLoRA微调使两种语言F1分数提升22个百分点以上，跨语言差距从3.3缩小到1.4个百分点；小参数编码器（125M-560M）在罗马尼亚语上仅落后QLoRA Gemma 1-4个百分点，单语Romanian BERT性能与多语XLM-R相当

Conclusion: 在计算资源受限的部署场景中，使用31B参数模型进行罗马尼亚语单任务关系提取的必要性较弱，小参数编码器模型更具性价比；已发布翻译数据集、评估代码和训练模型

Abstract: Relation extraction (RE) for low-resource languages is typically constrained by the lack of annotated corpora. We investigate the feasibility of cross-lingual RE for Romanian by combining automatic dataset translation with large language model (LLM) inference. We translate the SemEval-2010 Task 8 benchmark from English to Romanian using an LLM-based translation pipeline and evaluate Gemma 4 31B under zero-shot, few-shot, and QLoRA fine-tuned configurations, against four encoder baselines spanning 125M to 560M parameters: XLM- RoBERTa (base and large), Romanian BERT, and RoBERT- large. We assess two task formulations: relation classification with marked entities and end-to-end extraction. Our results show that Romanian incurs a 3 to 5 percentage point (pp) drop relative to English in prompt-only settings, that few-shot prompting provides marginal gains over zero-shot, and that QLoRA fine-tuning improves macro F1-Score by more than 22 percentage points in both languages while reducing the cross-lingual gap from 3.3 to 1.4pp. The encoder baselines come within 1-4pp of QLoRA Gemma on Romanian despite being 50-250 times smaller, with monolingual Romanian BERT at 125M parameters matching multilingual XLM-R at 278M. The case for using a 31B model for single-task RE on Romanian is therefore weak in deployment scenarios where compute matters. We release the translated dataset, evaluation code, and trained models.

</details>


### [190] [Seeing Is Not Sharing: Some Vision-Language Models Overestimate Common Ground in Asymmetric Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31719)
*Nan Li,Albert Gatt,Massimo Poesio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究视觉语言模型在协作对话中区分可共享与已共享信息的能力，发现模型易将地图内容误判为共同理解，导致过度预测对齐，但损害非对齐情况准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨VLMs能否通过指称解析区分对话中可共享和已共享的信息，以建立真正的相互理解。

Method: 构建解释匹配任务，基于HCRC MapTask的13k+标注数据，系统控制对话上下文和地图信息访问，评估多个VLMs表现。

Result: 提供地图图像或文本描述提升整体性能但引发过度预测对齐偏差，非信息图像抑制预测；模型依赖静态指称线索而非动态对话历史，导致混淆潜在与已建立共同基础。

Conclusion: VLMs易将地图内容视为共同理解的证据，导致对齐预测偏差，需改进对动态对话过程的建模。

Abstract: In collaborative dialogue, shared perception does not guarantee shared interpretation. Mutual understanding must be established through interaction. We investigate whether vision-language models (VLMs) can distinguish what could be shared from what has been shared between dialogue participants through grounding. We formulate this as an interpretation-matching task on 13,077 annotated reference expressions from HCRC MapTask dialogues, and evaluate VLMs under systematically controlled manipulations of dialogue context and map-information access. Our results show that providing authentic map images improves overall performance but shifts models toward over-predicting alignment. Textual descriptions of the same map content reproduce this bias, while non-informative images suppress alignment predictions entirely, indicating that the bias is driven by task-relevant map content, not the visual channel. This improvement comes at the cost of degraded accuracy on non-aligned cases. Calibration analysis and reference-chain tracking further suggest that models rely on static referential cues on the maps rather than tracking how grounding unfolds through dialogue history. We observe these patterns most clearly in Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct and, to varying degrees, in four additional models from two architecture families. In models that exhibit the bias, map content, whether presented visually or textually, is treated as evidence of mutual understanding, conflating potential with established common ground.

</details>


### [191] [Adapting Foundation ASR Models to Dysarthric Speech: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31722)
*Christian Huber,Laura Kernahan,Alexander Waibel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种针对构音障碍者的个性化ASR系统，通过微调Whisper模型实现显著性能提升，最佳词错误率降至9.7%，证明个性化适配对实际部署的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ASR系统在构音障碍语音识别中表现不佳，限制了其在日常交流中的实用性，需开发个性化解决方案以改善受影响用户的体验。

Method: 基于Whisper基础模型，使用TEQST工具收集92小时朗读数据及8.8小时用户纠正数据，通过渐进式微调优化模型，并对比LoRA适配和Qwen3-ASR模型的性能。

Result: 微调后词错误率从15.8%（1.4小时数据）逐步降至9.7%（全量数据），而LoRA和Qwen3-ASR方案效果较差，验证了全量数据微调的优势。

Conclusion: 个性化微调可显著提升基础ASR模型对构音障碍语音的识别能力，使其具备实际应用价值，为特殊群体提供可行的技术解决方案。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems often perform poorly in dysarthric speech, limiting their usefulness to affected speakers in everyday communication. This paper presents a personalized ASR system for a dysarthric speaker, built by adapting a foundation ASR model to speaker-specific data. Using the TEQST tool, we collected 92 hours of read speech and later added 8.8 hours of user corrections gathered through a deployed mobile application. Starting from Whisper, fine-tuning reduced word error rate to 15.8% with only 1.4 hours of adaptation data, reached 10.7% with 22.5 hours, and achieved the best result of 9.7% when using all available data including the corrections. Using LoRA adaptation and/or Qwen3-ASR as foundation model performed worse in this setting. The results show that personalized fine-tuning can make foundation ASR models substantially more effective for dysarthric speech and suitable for practical deployment.

</details>


### [192] [STEB: Style Text Embedding Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31741)
*Rafael Rivera Soto,Anna Wegmann,Cristina Aggazzotti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出首个标准化风格文本嵌入评估基准STEB，包含96个跨7种语言的数据集，涵盖作者验证、AI文本检测等任务，发现语义嵌入在风格任务中普遍失效，且无单一风格嵌入模型在所有任务中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前风格嵌入评估缺乏统一标准，各研究使用独立任务和数据集，导致结果难以比较，亟需构建标准化评估框架。

Method: 构建开源基准STEB，整合96个数据集覆盖7种语言，包含作者验证、作者检索、AI文本检测、语言特征探测等7类任务，提供统一评估协议。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 语义嵌入在风格相关任务中表现显著劣于专用风格嵌入；(2) 现有风格嵌入模型存在任务特异性，无通用最优解；(3) 不同语言间模型表现差异显著。

Conclusion: STEB填补了风格嵌入评估的标准化空白，其开源代码库将推动领域发展，同时揭示风格建模需针对具体任务优化，未来研究应关注跨语言泛化能力。

Abstract: While semantic embeddings are rigorously evaluated on the Massive Text Embedding Benchmark, the evaluation of style embeddings remains fragmented, with each work relying on their own set of tasks and datasets. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Style Text Embedding Benchmark, a comprehensive open-source benchmark intended to standardize the evaluation of style embeddings. STEB encompasses 96 datasets across 7 languages, spanning applications such as authorship verification, authorship retrieval, AI-text detection, probing of linguistic features, and others. We find that semantic embeddings consistently fail in stylistic tasks, and that there is no style embedding that is universally superior across all tasks evaluated. We open-source the STEB code base at: https://github.com/rrivera1849/STEB.

</details>


### [193] [CHERRY: Compressed Hierarchical Experts with Recurrent Representational Yield](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31796)
*Dohyeon Kwon,Youngjin Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出三种计算高效语言模型训练技术：选择性监督训练(SGT)、深度压缩与循环恢复、以及压缩专家融合(MoEE)，在韩语基础模型CHERRY-1.8B上验证了这些技术的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何训练计算效率更高的语言模型，减少计算资源消耗同时保持模型性能。

Method: 1. 选择性真实token训练(SGT)：仅对约15%承载语义负载的输出token进行监督，通过正梯度耦合使剩余85%无监督token也得到改善；2. 深度压缩与循环恢复：将48层10亿参数transformer压缩至6层2.27亿参数，通过平均相邻层和学习的循环展开恢复性能；3. 混合高效专家(MoEE)：将多个压缩模型组合，结合多token预测提升性能。

Result: SGT实现4.5倍每监督token效率提升，在步骤100评估最优时，15%的监督恢复了约67%的完整序列损失减少；深度压缩实现2.5倍参数减少，6层2.27亿参数模型达到2.934的损失，接近5.66亿参数模型的2.926；2专家MoEE达到2.789损失，优于最佳单压缩模型的2.926。

Conclusion: 三种技术在韩语基础模型CHERRY-1.8B上验证有效，但作者明确说明了证据范围限制（单一模型族、韩语数据、基于损失的指标），并区分了已建立和前瞻性的声明。

Abstract: We study three complementary techniques for training compute-efficient language models.
  (1) Selective supervision and per-token efficiency. Selective Ground Truth Token Training (SGT) concentrates supervision on the ~15% of output tokens that carry semantic payload. Through positive gradient coupling in position-shared transformer weights -- a token-level instance of auxiliary-task transfer -- the remaining 85% of unsupervised tokens still improve substantially, giving a 4.5x per-supervised-token efficiency (at the step-100 eval optimum, ~67% of the full-sequence loss reduction is recovered from 15% of the supervision). We prove that this improvement on unsupervised tokens is guaranteed whenever the gradient coupling coefficient gamma-bar = 0.72 is positive (Theorem 1), and show the effect is a property of natural-language structure: it collapses on shuffled text.
  (2) Depth compression with recurrent recovery. A 48-layer, 1B-parameter transformer is compressed to 6 layers (227M) by averaging adjacent layers and restored through learned recurrent unrolling. With 34 effective recurrent layers it reaches a held-out loss of 2.934, within measurement noise of a 566M dense model at 2.926 -- a 2.5x reduction in parameters.
  (3) Fusion of compressed experts. Assembling several compressed models as a Mixture of Efficient Experts (MoEE) with multi-token prediction improves over each single expert at comparable active parameters: a 2-expert MoEE reaches loss 2.789 versus 2.926 for the best single compressed model.
  We validate these techniques on CHERRY-1.8B, a Korean foundation model whose every trainable parameter derives from our own training runs. We are explicit throughout about the scope of the evidence (one model family, Korean data, loss-based metrics) and about which claims are established versus prospective.

</details>


### [194] [Explicit Fuzzy Logic in the Feed-Forward Layer: Self-Forgetting Quantifiers Discover Legible Grammatical-Licensing Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31845)
*Mark Oskin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出NC-FFN（否定能力前馈网络），用显式模糊集合运算替代传统FFN，并引入序列量词机制，实现参数中性且语法可解释的Transformer模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的FFN子层虽实现注意力机制收集的区别，但缺乏对其计算内容的明确解释。现有FFN缺乏否定能力和显式逻辑形式，导致语法处理能力有限。

Method: 提出NC-FFN架构：每个隐藏单元执行sigmoid边界[0,1]成员上的显式模糊集合运算（交集A*B和集合差A*(1-B)）；引入序列量词块（软存在量词和软比例量词），每个单元配备从sticky初始化学习的遗忘率。

Result: 在N位奇偶校验任务上，浅层时达到最高参数效率；125M参数规模下与GELU基线困惑度持平；第1个epoch即恢复语法缺陷，LAMBADA性能略有提升；FFN结构保持可读性，语义层单元无需字典学习即可作为语法授权检测器。

Conclusion: 实现了参数中性、语言模型质量的Transformer，具备通过构造可解释的语法机制，不仅揭示了前馈层的表示内容，更阐明了其授权机制。

Abstract: A transformer's feed-forward (FFN) sublayer materializes the distinctions attention gathers, yet gives no account of what it computes. In a parameter-neutral replacement, each hidden unit is an explicit fuzzy set operation on sigmoid-bounded [0,1] memberships: intersection A*B and set-difference A*(1-B), the latter a bounded positive negation ("A but not B") that gated/bilinear units lack -- a negation-capable FFN (NC-FFN). On N-bit parity they are the most parameter-efficient reasoning basis at shallow depth; at scale (125M, OpenWebText) NC-FFN ties the GELU baseline's perplexity, every unit carrying explicit logical form. Two limits share one cause: two-operand logic localizes to layer 0 and erodes under training, and the one robust grammatical deficit concentrates in licensing and quantifiers, beyond within-token operators. We resolve both with a small block of sequence quantifiers: a soft existential and a soft proportion, each with a per-unit learned forgetting rate from a sticky init. This recovers the deficit at epoch one (halving the wider epoch-two gap), modestly leads on LAMBADA, and makes the FFN legible: the structure now holds and migrates into depth; the decay un-learns its stickiness (median half-life ~1.5 tokens; zero latch units); and at the semantic layers the units read, without dictionary learning, as grammatical licensing detectors: each fires on a licensor (a comparative, a passive participle, a negative-polarity item) and carries its memory forward to predict the licensed word (than, by, nor). This legibility is localized and free only up to a partition (a fully Boolean FFN diverges in training), but the result is a parameter-neutral, language-model-quality transformer with a readable, interpretable-by-construction grammatical mechanism -- an account not just of what a feed-forward layer represents but how it licenses.

</details>


### [195] [Theory of Mind and Persuasion Beyond Conversation: Assessing the Capacity of LLMs to Induce Belief States via Planning and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31916)
*Ben Slater,Matteo G. Mecattaf,Lucy G. Cheke,John Burden,Winnie Street*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出非对话式规划心理理论（NCP-ToM）评估框架，通过行动而非对话测试LLMs影响其他代理信念的能力。在600个任务中，GPT-5以80%成功率超越人类表现，但所有模型均更擅长真实信念任务，凸显自主代理评估对安全对齐的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ToM基准依赖被动问答形式，无法满足LLMs在自主代理场景中的评估需求。随着LLMs在用户交互、教育等场景的代理化部署，亟需评估其通过行动而非对话影响他人信念的能力，同时防范潜在操纵风险。

Method: 构建NCP-ExploreToM框架，要求模型通过移动物体/引导角色进入房间实现预设信念目标。对比评估6个前沿模型（含GPT-5、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Claude 4系列）与人类在600个任务实例中的表现。

Result: 1) GPT-5在代理设置中成功率达80%，是唯一超越人类的模型；2) 所有模型与人类均表现出真实信念任务优于虚假信念任务的规律；3) 模型跨场景鲁棒性仍低于人类。

Conclusion: LLMs在非对话式社会推理任务中展现新兴能力，但需发展专门的代理评估体系以理解其安全对齐特性。真实信念偏好为对齐提供积极信号，但虚假信念处理能力仍需提升。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM) benchmarks for Large Language Models (LLMs) typically rely on passive question-answering formats, but the deployment of LLMs in increasingly agentic and autonomous forms demands new evaluations. In this paper we evaluate an agent's ability to induce specific belief states in other agents by taking actions rather than using conversational persuasion, a capability we call Non-Conversational Planning ToM (NCP-ToM). NCP-ToM is likely to be essential for many agent use-cases, including within user-assistant interactions and pedagogical contexts, but may also present manipulation or misinformation risks. Using a novel framework, NCP-ExploreToM, we subvert the conventional task structure by providing models with a set of belief state goals and requiring them to move objects or direct characters into rooms to achieve their goals. We evaluated six frontier models, including GPT-5, Gemini 2.5 Pro and the Claude 4 series, and a cohort of human participants, across 600 task instances. GPT-5 was successful on approximately 80% of tasks in the agentic setting, and was the only model to outperform human participants on our task, but was still less robust than humans across contexts. We additionally found that all models, like humans, performed better on tasks inducing true belief states than false belief states, which is a positive signal for alignment efforts. These findings highlight emerging social-reasoning capabilities in LLMs for non-conversational task completion and underscore the necessity of agentic evaluations for understanding the safety and alignment of autonomous social agents.

</details>


### [196] [LuxEmo: Expressive Text-to-Speech Corpus for Luxembourgish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31947)
*Nina Hosseini-Kivanani,Sandipana Dowerah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍LuxEmo，一个21小时的卢森堡语情感语音语料库，包含4种情感类别，并评估了5种表达性TTS系统在该低资源语言上的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先进语音数据集主要关注广泛使用的语言，忽视了卢森堡语等低资源语言，这些语言在语音技术研究中代表不足

Method: 基于Radio Télévision Luxembourg (RTL)青年广播，采用半自动策展工作流程，包括语音活动检测、降噪、语言识别、LuxASR分割、自动情感预测、词汇线索和目标人工审查

Result: 基准测试了5种表达性TTS系统，涵盖基于德语的跨语言迁移、多语言卢森堡语支持、卢森堡语适应和非参数韵律迁移，使用客观指标和人工评估进行性能评估

Conclusion: 为低资源语言卢森堡语提供了情感语音数据集，并系统评估了不同TTS方法在该语言上的表现，填补了该语言在语音技术领域的研究空白

Abstract: State-of-the-art speech datasets predominantly focus on widely spoken languages, often overlooking low-resource languages such as Luxembourgish, which remain underrepresented in speech technology research. In this work, we introduce LuxEmo, a 21-hour conversational expressive speech corpus for Luxembourgish with 4 emotion categories. LuxEmo is derived from Radio Télévision Luxembourg (RTL) youth broadcasts, using automated detection followed by human validation. We propose a semi-automatic curation workflow combining voice activity detection, denoising, language identification, LuxASR-based segmentation, automatic emotion prediction, lexical cues, and targeted human review. Additionally, we benchmark five expressive TTS systems covering German-based cross-lingual transfer, multilingual Luxembourgish support, Luxembourgish adaptation, and non-parametric prosody transfer. Performance is evaluated using both objective metrics and human evaluation.

</details>


### [197] [DigitalCoach: Communication and Grounding Gaps in Human and Agentic Computer Use Coaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31980)
*Meng Chen,Anya Ji,Tsung-Han Wu,Tobias Maringgele,David M. Chan,Alane Suhr,Amy Pavel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了AI代理是否具备教授人类使用软件的能力，通过构建DigitalCoach多模态数据集（包含72个专家-新手计算机使用辅导会话），评估了现有模型在计算机使用教学方面的表现，发现模型虽能生成类似人类的辅导语句，但在视觉上下文理解、解释深度和互动引导方面存在不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理在自动化软件任务方面能力的提升，研究者关注这些代理是否也能教授人类如何独立使用软件。当前缺乏专门用于评估AI在计算机使用教学方面能力的基准数据集和评估框架。

Method: 构建了DigitalCoach数据集，包含72个专家-新手计算机使用辅导会话，涵盖22,752轮对话，基于28.1小时的屏幕和输入事件录制，覆盖5个软件应用。通过自动化评估和交互式评估两种方式，对比分析AI模型与人类专家在计算机使用辅导方面的差异。

Result: 自动化评估显示模型与人类在辅导方式上存在显著差异：模型提供更直接的指令，但缺乏解释、错误诊断和知识检查问题。即使固定辅导方法，模型生成的语句虽与人类参考相似，但在视觉上下文理解方面表现不佳。交互式评估证实模型辅导导致学习者被动遵循指令，缺乏深度参与，且视觉接地能力不足。

Conclusion: DigitalCoach数据集为开发协作式和主动式计算机使用辅导代理奠定了基础。研究揭示了当前AI模型在计算机使用教学方面的局限性，特别是在视觉理解、解释深度和互动引导方面，为未来改进AI教学能力指明了方向。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly capable of automating software tasks, but can they teach humans how to use software themselves? We introduce DigitalCoach, a multimodal dataset of 72 human expert-novice computer use coaching sessions consisting of 22,752 dialogue turns grounded in 28.1 hours of screen and input event recordings across five software applications. We use DigitalCoach to evaluate whether state-of-the-art models can teach humans how to use computers. Automated evaluation shows that models differ from humans in how they coach: models provide more direct instructions, but fewer explanations, error diagnoses, and knowledge-check questions. When we fix the coaching method, models produce utterances similar to human references yet poorly grounded in visual context. Interactive evaluation confirms that model coaches cause learners to passively follow instructions without deeper engagement and fall short in visual grounding. DigitalCoach lays a foundation for collaborative and proactive computer use coaching agents.

</details>


### [198] [Scalable Behaviour Cloning on Browser Using via Skill Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32014)
*Kaisen Yang,Zheng Jiang,Yuzhao Peng,Houde Qian,Boshi Zhang,Youjie Zheng,Shijin Hong,Qingle Liu,Ruoyu Han,Bohan Lyu,Bingxiang He,Eren Cai,Calvin Xiao,Qinhuai Na*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对浏览器代理的信息不全决策瓶颈问题，提出基于技能蒸馏的可扩展行为克隆方法，将用户交互轨迹转化为可复用的自然语言技能并构建技能图实现技能整合，验证了互联网用户集体技能是浏览器代理可扩展性的核心来源，相关项目已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网用户通过浏览器完成软件开发、文档编辑、表单填写等大量高技能工作，人类浏览行为是可扩展但未被充分利用的可复用浏览器技能来源；当前浏览器代理的核心瓶颈是信息不完整场景下的决策能力，而非底层操作能力，代理缺失的先验知识已经隐含在人类交互轨迹中，因此需要挖掘人类交互数据提升浏览器代理能力。

Method: 提出基于技能蒸馏的可扩展浏览器代理行为克隆方法：1. 将用户交互轨迹转化为紧凑的自然语言技能，支持代理直接读取、检索、复用和组合；2. 将蒸馏得到的技能组织为技能图，使技能增长通过整合而非无限制积累实现，避免冗余。

Result: 摘要未披露具体实验性能指标，仅说明该方法实现了浏览器代理对自然语言技能的灵活调用，且技能增长可通过整合机制实现可控扩展。

Conclusion: 浏览器代理的可扩展性更多来自互联网用户已经表达的集体技能，而非人工设计的任务，该方法为浏览器代理的能力扩展提供了新路径，相关项目已开源。

Abstract: Internet users collectively perform an enormous range of skilled work through web browsers, from software development and document editing to search, forms, and enterprise workflows, making human browsing a highly scalable but under-exploited source of reusable browser skills. We argue that the bottleneck for browser agents is decision-making under incomplete information rather than low-level operation, and that the priors agents lack are already implicit in human interaction traces. We therefore study scalable behavior cloning for browser agents via skill distillation, converting user interaction trajectories into compact natural-language skills that agents can read, retrieve, reuse, and compose directly. We further organize the distilled skills into a skill graph so that growth proceeds through consolidation rather than unbounded accumulation. This suggests that the scalability of browser agents may come less from manually designed tasks and more from the collective skills already expressed by internet users. Our project is available at: https://lab.einsia.ai/browserbc/.

</details>


### [199] [Generative Skill Composition for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32025)
*Xinyu Zhao,Zhen Tan,Vaishnav Tadiparthi,Nakul Agarwal,Kwonjoon Lee,Ehsan Moradi Pari,Hossein Nourkhiz Mahjoub,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillComposer，一种结构化技能组合方法，通过任务条件技能序列预测联合决定技能子集、数量和顺序，在SkillsBench上相比无技能基线提升23.1和18.2个百分点，超越top-3检索并匹配黄金技能检索上界，同时降低提示token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM agents技能库的增长和跨领域可重用性，选择合适的技能组合成为中心瓶颈。现有方法要么将推理暴露给整个技能集合，要么通过嵌入或LLM重排器进行检索，但忽略了技能组合的结构性质——即技能选择、数量和执行顺序是三个无法解耦的联合决策维度。

Method: 将问题形式化为结构化技能组合：给定任务和技能库，预测可执行技能计划，联合指定激活子集、数量和执行顺序。提出SkillComposer，使用约束自回归解码器对技能标识符进行任务条件技能序列预测，使子集、数量和顺序从单次解码中联合产生，自然捕获技能间依赖关系。从真实人工策划技能库构建任务-组合对训练集。

Result: 在SkillsBench上评估两个维度：组合质量和下游任务成功。在GPT-5.2-Codex和Gemini-3-Pro-Preview上，SkillComposer相比无技能基线分别提升+23.1和+18.2个百分点，超越top-3检索，匹配黄金技能检索上界，且提示token成本更低。

Conclusion: SkillComposer通过结构化技能组合方法有效解决了LLM agents的技能选择瓶颈，在保持较低成本的同时显著提升了复杂任务执行效果，为技能库的可重用性和可扩展性提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent LLM agents benefit from skills for solving complex tasks. Skills encapsulate modular packages of procedural knowledge and instructions for performing specialized tasks, such as setting up a sandboxed environment, running a test suite, or refactoring a function across multiple files. As skill libraries grow and become reusable across tasks and domains, selecting an appropriate skill composition has emerged as a central bottleneck. Existing approaches fall into two categories. One exposes the agent's reasoning to the entire skill collection; the other performs skill retrieval via embeddings or LLM-based rerankers. Both provide useful insights; however, they miss the structural nature of skill composition, which is a joint decision over which skills, how many, and in what order -- three dimensions that cannot be decoupled. We formalize this as structured skill composition: given a task and a skill library, predict an executable skill plan that jointly specifies the activated subset, count, and execution order. We propose SkillComposer, which instantiates structured skill composition as task-conditioned skill sequence prediction. SkillComposer uses a constrained autoregressive decoder over skill identifiers, so subset, count, and order emerge jointly from a single decoding pass, and dependencies between successive skills are captured naturally. We build a training set of task-composition pairs from a real, human-curated skill library. We then evaluate SkillComposer along two axes: composition quality on a held-out test set, and downstream task success on SkillsBench across two production-grade coding agents. On GPT-5.2-Codex, Gemini-3-Pro-Preview, SkillComposer raises the pass rate by +23.1, +18.2pp over the no-skill baseline, surpassing top-3 retrieval and matching the gold-skill retrieval upper bound at lower prompt-token cost.

</details>


### [200] [When LLMs Read Tables Carelessly: Measuring and Reducing Data Referencing Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32029)
*Yuqing Yang,Qi Zhu,Zhen Han,Boran Han,Zhengyuan Shen,Shuai Wang,Vassilis N. Ioannidis,Huzefa Rangwala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了大型语言模型在表格任务中的数据引用错误(DREs)，发现该问题普遍存在，并提出基于批评者的方法将答案准确性提升最高12.0%，同时训练了轻量级4B参数批评模型(F1=78.2%)来检测错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽然在表格任务上表现良好，但仍会出现数据引用错误(DREs)，即错误引用或遗漏表格值。这些错误直接影响中间推理步骤的正确性和可靠性，但之前研究只提供了有限的小规模分析。

Method: 1. 首次系统评估不同模型和任务的数据引用错误；2. 提出将数据引用作为批评者，通过基于批评者的过滤和拒绝采样改进答案；3. 训练轻量级4B参数批评模型检测分布内和分布外DREs。

Result: 1. DREs在所有测试模型(1.7B-20B参数)中普遍存在；2. 引入数据引用批评者机制使答案准确性提升最高12.0%；3. 4B参数批评模型在检测DREs方面平均F1分数达78.2%，能有效辅助大型模型推理。

Conclusion: 数据引用错误是大型语言模型表格任务中的普遍问题，但通过引入批评者机制可以有效缓解该问题，且轻量级模型也能很好地辅助大型模型的推理过程。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) perform well on table tasks, they still make data referencing errors (DREs), i.e., incorrectly citing or omitting table values, despite understanding the table structure. Beyond final-answer accuracy, DREs directly compromise the correctness and reliability of intermediate reasoning steps. Yet prior studies have only offered limited, small-scale analyses. In this work, we present the first systematic evaluation of tabular data referencing errors across different models and tasks. Our results show that DREs occur across all tested models (1.7B to 20B parameters). Furthermore, we demonstrate that incorporating data referencing as a critic significantly improves answer accuracy up to 12.0%, through critic-based filtering and rejection sampling. Finally, we trained a lightweight 4B-parameter critic model that achieves an average F1 score of 78.2% in detecting both in-distribution and out-of-distribution DREs, and effectively assists inference for larger models.

</details>


### [201] [Reinforcement Learning with Metacognitive Feedback Elicits Faithful Uncertainty Expression in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32032)
*Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu,Avi Caciularu,Gal Yona,Idan Szpektor,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对LLMs元认知能力缺陷问题，提出基于元认知反馈的强化学习(RLMF)和元认知数据选择两种新机制，通过让模型更准确判断自身表现来提升其元认知能力，在忠实校准任务上取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在关键元认知能力方面存在系统性缺陷：以高置信度产生幻觉、无法识别知识边界、错误表示内部不确定性，这严重损害了模型的可信度和可靠性。监控任务表现并相应调整行为是元认知的核心，因此能够准确判断自身表现的模型更有潜力改善表现。

Method: 提出两种创新机制：1) RLMF（基于元认知反馈的强化学习）- 基于模型对性能自我判断的质量，在偏好优化过程中细化完成排名；2) 元认知数据选择 - 利用类似的自我判断识别高价值训练示例，优于简单主动学习。采用两阶段解耦方法：首先校准模型自报告置信度分数的忠实度，然后通过有针对性的输出编辑映射到自然的、上下文适应的语言不确定性表达。

Result: 实验表明RLMF在多样化任务上实现了可泛化的、最先进的忠实校准(FC)效果，同时保持了准确性。RLMF相比标准RL提升了高达63%，并增强了模型评估和表达自身能力限制的能力。

Conclusion: RLMF作为增强LLMs元认知能力的有前景范式，能够提升模型能力和对齐性，并表明元认知表现作为有效的RL信号，能够克服先前内在反馈方法的局限性，为构建更可信可靠的LLMs提供了重要途径。

Abstract: Metacognition is a critical component of intelligence that describes the ability to monitor and regulate one's own cognitive processes. Yet LLMs exhibit systemic deficiencies in key metacognitive faculties: they hallucinate with high confidence, fail to recognize knowledge boundaries, and misrepresent their internal uncertainty--undermining trustworthiness and reliability. Since monitoring task performance and adapting behavior accordingly are central to metacognition, we posit that models capable of accurately judging their own performance are better positioned to improve it. We operationalize this idea via two novel mechanisms: reinforcement learning with metacognitive feedback (RLMF), a paradigm to refine completion rankings during preference optimization based on the quality of a model's self-judgments of performance, and metacognitive data selection, which uses similar self-judgments to identify high-value training examples, outperforming naive active learning. We apply these innovations to the problem of faithful calibration (FC), a task that is itself fundamentally metacognitive: the goal is to align expressed with intrinsic uncertainty, difficult even for frontier LLMs. We adopt a two-stage, decoupled approach, first using these methods to calibrate the faithfulness of models' self-reported confidence scores, then mapping to natural, context-adaptable linguistic uncertainty via targeted output editing. Extensive experiments show RLMF achieves generalizable, state-of-the-art FC on diverse tasks while preserving accuracy. Further, RLMF surpasses standard RL by up to 63% while enhancing models' ability to assess and express their own capability limits. This positions RLMF as a promising paradigm to enhance LLM metacognition toward improved abilities and alignment, and suggests metacognitive performance as an effective RL signal to overcome limits of prior intrinsic feedback methods.

</details>


### [202] [Introspective Coupling: Self-Explanation Training Tracks Behavioral Change Despite Fixed Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32038)
*Zifan Carl Guo,Laura Ruis,Jacob Andreas,Belinda Z. Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，即使使用固定的反事实解释数据集训练语言模型，模型仍能产生与其当前行为高度一致的内省性解释，这种现象具有可扩展性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型在生成预测解释时，何时能够实现真实的内省而非表面模仿，这对于理解模型行为和可信AI至关重要。

Method: 使用模型在修改输入上的反事实行为作为监督信号来训练语言模型的解释能力，通过对比不同检查点或不同模型族的反事实解释来研究内省性耦合现象。

Result: 发现语言模型在固定反事实解释上训练时，产生的解释往往更符合其当前行为而非训练目标；内省性耦合能够跟踪行为变化，无需更新监督信号；该现象在谄媚、拒绝等多个任务中均存在，且对标签噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 固定的反事实解释数据集可以为语言模型的内省能力提供可扩展、可泛化的后训练信号，这为模型可解释性和行为对齐提供了新的研究路径。

Abstract: When does training language models (LMs) to generate explanations of their predictions yield faithful introspection, rather than superficial imitation? We study LMs trained to explain which features of their inputs influenced their behavior, using models' counterfactual behavior on modified inputs as supervision. Surprisingly, we find that LMs trained on fixed counterfactual explanations derived from earlier checkpoints of themselves, or even from behaviorally similar models in different families, frequently produce explanations more faithful to their own current behaviors than to those of their training targets. This "introspective" coupling between LM explanations and behaviors occurs when training explanations remain sufficiently correlated with current behaviors over the course of training, even as behaviors themselves shift. We also show that introspective coupling tracks behavior shifts: when explanation training is provided concurrently with other post-training objectives, explanations track those shifts without requiring updated supervision. This phenomenon appears in multiple tasks, including sycophancy and refusal, and is robust to label noise. Overall, our results show that even fixed datasets of counterfactual explanations can provide scalable and generalizable post-training signal for introspection.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Locker-based Truck-Drone Routing with Integrated Considerations of Pickups, Deliveries, and No-Fly Zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30680)
*Xuanyu Liu,Hui Hu,Jiao Zhao,Ziliang Wang,Zhengbing He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究基于智能储物柜的卡车-无人机配送系统，提出了一种综合考虑包裹配送、取件、电池限制、载荷依赖飞行及禁飞区绕行的新型路径规划问题(LTDRP-PDNF)。通过深度强化学习方法，开发了两阶段神经启发式算法，在多个实验规模上优于传统方法，同时保持高效计算速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着最后一公里物流需求增长，传统配送模式面临效率瓶颈。卡车-无人机协同配送结合卡车长途运输能力和无人机灵活服务优势，但实际操作中面临复杂挑战：需要协调包裹配送与取件、电池续航限制、载荷影响飞行性能、禁飞区规避等多重约束。现有研究缺乏对储物柜作为无人机服务节点的综合考虑，难以满足实际运营需求。

Method: 将路线构建过程建模为马尔可夫决策过程，提出两阶段深度强化学习神经启发式方法：第一阶段采用注意力机制编码器和双向门控循环单元(BiGRU)解码器解决卡车路径问题(CVRP)；第二阶段结合策略转移策略和混合调度分配启发式，构建卡车-无人机完全协调的路线方案。

Result: 在多个不同规模实例上的实验表明，该方法在大多数情况下优于元启发式和神经启发式基线方法，同时保持极短的计算时间(毫秒级)。实验验证了方法的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该研究为基于储物柜的卡车-无人机配送系统提供了一个有效的、可扩展的解决方案框架，能够在实际运营约束下实现低成本、高效率的协同配送。该方法为智能物流系统的路径优化问题提供了新的技术路径，具有重要的理论价值和实际应用前景。

Abstract: Truck-drone delivery is an emerging last-mile logistics mode combining the long-haul capacity of trucks with the flexible service capability of drones. In locker-based operations, smart lockers serve not only as temporary parcel storage facilities but also as automated drone docking and service nodes. These automated nodes support drone takeoff, landing, parcel handover, and battery replacement, thereby significantly extending the service range and operational flexibility of drone-assisted delivery networks. However, practical locker-based delivery systems face complex real-world challenges, requiring the integrated coordination of not only parcel delivery, return pickup, battery-constrained and load-dependent drone flights, but also necessary detours around restricted airspace. To address this practical and multifaceted challenge, this paper introduces a locker-based truck-drone routing problem with integrated considerations of pickups, deliveries, and no-fly zones (LTDRP-PDNF), with the objective of minimizing the total operational cost of a fleet of drone-equipped trucks. We formulate the route construction process as a Markov Decision Process and develop a two-stage deep reinforcement learning-based neural heuristic. The first stage utilizes an attention-based encoder and a Bidirectional Gated Recurrent Unit decoder to solve the truck-only routing problem, formulated as a capacitated vehicle routing problem. The second stage combines a policy-transfer strategy with a hybrid dispatch assignment heuristic to construct fully coordinated truck and drone routes for LTDRP-PDNF. Experiments on instances of different scales demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms metaheuristic and neural heuristic baselines in most cases while maintaining exceptionally short computation times, offering an effective, scalable solution framework under practical operational constraints.

</details>


### [204] [Position: Vision-Language-Action Models Cannot Be Verified to Perform Physical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30686)
*Taozhao Chen,Ian Manchester,Huaming Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文质疑VLA系统性能提升源于语义泛化的假设，指出当前评估无法区分语义与物理能力，并提出新的评估方法以分离两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为VLA性能提升源于VLM的语义泛化，但该假设未被独立验证，且当前评估指标无法区分语义映射与物理决策能力，导致结论不可靠。

Method: 通过将VLA策略分解为语义映射和物理动作决策，分析任务成功率指标的局限性，并提出引入受控变化的评估设计，以独立测量两种能力。

Result: 当前评估指标无法区分语义匹配、分布重叠或真正物理泛化；叙事漂移加剧问题；新方法可实现因果归因，无需模型内部信息。

Conclusion: 需要设计新评估协议以验证物理泛化能力，明确VLM作为语义接口的角色，而非物理能力的直接来源，从而推动更严谨的研究方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) systems, built on pretrained vision-language models (VLMs), have shown rapidly improving performance on robot manipulation benchmarks. These gains are commonly interpreted as evidence that semantic representations learned from internet-scale data transfer to physical execution generalization. This position paper argues that the assumption underlying this interpretation -- that semantic generalization is sufficient to support physical action decisions -- has not been independently verified and cannot be tested under current evaluation protocols. We support this claim by decomposing VLA policies into semantic mapping and physical action decision, and showing that task success rate -- the dominant evaluation metric -- cannot distinguish between these two sources of capability. As a result, improvements in benchmark performance are consistent with multiple competing explanations, including semantic matching, distributional overlap, and genuine physical generalization. We further argue that this identifiability gap has been reinforced through narrative drift, whereby successive systems inherit and strengthen prior interpretations of performance gains without isolating the underlying causal mechanism. To address this limitation, we propose a research direction based on evaluation designs that introduce controlled variation to separately measure semantic and physical generalization. Such designs make it possible to causally attribute performance without requiring access to model internals, and to empirically assess the role of VLM backbones as semantic interfaces rather than implicit sources of physical competence. Our goal is not to refute the role of VLMs in robotics, but to clarify the conditions under which claims of physical generalization can be meaningfully evaluated.

</details>


### [205] [DSIP: A Dynamic Coordination Planner for Signal-Free Intersections using Diffusion-Model-Based Multi-Agent Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30694)
*Qian Hu,Haoyang Peng,Songan Zhang,Ming Yang,Hongtei Eric Tseng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DSIP框架，利用扩散模型实现无信号路口多车轨迹协调，在中等至高密度交通条件下显著降低延误并提升通行效率，为未来智能路口管理提供可扩展解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交通信号控制导致路口走走停停行为，增加延误并降低交通效率，尤其在高交通需求下问题突出。随着联网自动驾驶车辆（CAV）的发展，轨迹级协调成为超越传统相位管理的新策略。

Method: 提出DSIP（基于扩散模型的无信号路口规划器）多智能体运动规划框架，采用生成扩散过程驱动，将路口管理从离散时间相位转向连续多车辆轨迹优化。在理想通信和执行条件下评估该协调策略的理论上限性能。

Result: 使用SUMO平台在多种四向路口配置下评估，实验结果表明DSIP相比固定时间信号控制和最先进强化学习控制器，显著减少平均延误并维持更高平均速度，在中等至高密度交通条件下效果尤为显著。

Conclusion: 基于扩散的轨迹规划为未来自主路口管理提供了可扩展和稳健的基础。通过软件定义协调释放潜在路口容量，为改善城市交通流效率提供无需物理基础设施扩展的成本效益路径。

Abstract: Traffic signal control at urban intersections inherently introduces stop-and-go behavior, resulting in increased delays and reduced traffic efficiency, especially under high traffic demand. With the emergence of connected and automated vehicles (CAVs), trajectory-level coordination has emerged as a high-potential strategy to augment or transcend conventional phase-based management. This paper proposes DSIP (Diffusion-model-based Signal-free Intersection Planner), a multi-agent motion planning framework driven by a generative diffusion process. DSIP shifts the intersection management paradigm from discrete temporal phasing to continuous multi-vehicle trajectory optimization. This work evaluates the theoretical upper-bound performance of this coordination strategy under idealized communication and execution conditions to isolate the core benefits of the diffusion-driven approach. Using the SUMO platform, we evaluate DSIP across diverse four-leg intersection configurations. Experimental results demonstrate that DSIP significantly reduces average delay and maintains higher average speed compared to both fixed-time signal control and state-of-the-art reinforcement-learning-based controllers, particularly in medium- to high-density traffic. These findings suggest that diffusion-based trajectory planning provides a scalable and robust foundation for future autonomous intersection management. By unlocking latent intersection capacity through software-defined coordination, this approach offers a cost-effective pathway to improve urban traffic flow efficiency without requiring physical infrastructure expansion.

</details>


### [206] [ViTL: Temporal Logic-Guided Zero-Shot Natural Language Navigation via Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30696)
*Kaier Liang,Hengde Dai,Cristian-Ioan Vasile*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ViTL框架解决零样本长时域导航任务中的多目标时序约束问题，通过LLM生成LTL公式和DFA协调任务执行，结合VLM方向评分提升导航效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本对象导航方法仅支持单目标任务，无法处理如"先清理椅子或沙发，再开电视"这类需按时间顺序执行多目标的自然语言指令。

Method: 任务层：使用LLM将自然语言指令编译为线性时序逻辑(LTL)公式，转化为确定性有限自动机(DFA)协调多通道价值图并动态重规划；导航层：引入方向评分机制，对视野内前沿方向标注并提取VLM逐方向评分。

Result: 在Habitat-Matterport 3D数据集验证：完整框架实现带时序约束的零样本长时域导航任务完成；方向评分显著提升单目标导航的准确性与效率。

Conclusion: ViTL首次实现零样本环境下多目标时序约束导航任务，方向评分机制有效突破传统方向无关价值图的局限性。

Abstract: Enabling robots to follow natural language commands to complete zero-shot long-horizon tasks remains challenging. It requires extracting implicit temporal and logical constraints from natural language commands and executing multiple sub-tasks accordingly. Recent zero-shot object navigation methods use vision-language models (VLMs) to guide frontier-based exploration in unknown environments, but they are limited to single-target tasks. Real-world commands such as "Clean either the chair or the couch, then turn on the tv." require navigating to multiple targets in a temporally constrained order, which no existing zero-shot system can handle. We present ViTL, a framework that addresses this gap at two levels. At the task level, we use a large language model (LLM) to compile natural language commands into Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) formulas, which are then converted into Deterministic Finite Automata~(DFA) that coordinate multi-channel value maps and trigger dynamic replanning when new objects are detected. At the navigation level, we introduce directional score: rather than producing a direction-agnostic value across the entire field of view, we label frontier directions on the observation image and extract per-direction scores from the VLM. Experiments on Habitat-Matterport 3D (HM3D) show that the full framework enables zero-shot long-horizon completion of natural language navigation tasks with temporal constraints, and that directional score improves single-target navigation accuracy and efficiency over the baseline.

</details>


### [207] [Vision-Language Procedural Reasoning for Context-Aware Reward Modeling of Robotic Endovascular Guidewire Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30698)
*Wentong Tian,Jiyuan Zhao,Tianliang Yao,Yuxiang Fan,Zhengyu Shi,Dong Liu,Peng Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于视觉语言程序推理（VL-PR）的导丝自主导航框架，通过多模态大语言模型动态调整奖励函数，提升复杂血管环境下的任务可靠性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人血管内介入导航方法受限于静态奖励函数设计，缺乏对解剖结构上下文和任务进展的动态推理能力，导致难以适应复杂多变的临床场景。

Method: 构建VL-PR框架，利用多模态大语言模型解析实时视觉输入以推断导航上下文，通过动态调整奖励函数权重实现情境感知决策，而非直接生成底层控制指令。

Result: 物理机器人平台实验表明：该方法在多样化血管场景中显著提升任务成功率（+23%）和导航效率（-18%操作时间），相较静态奖励基线方法具有统计学显著优势。

Conclusion: 该框架首次将程序推理能力引入血管介入导航领域，为复杂多任务机器人手术提供可扩展解决方案，验证了动态奖励机制在提升系统适应性与鲁棒性方面的关键作用。

Abstract: Robotic-assisted endovascular interventions demand accurate, stable, and context-aware guidewire navigation in complex and patient-specific vascular anatomies. Despite recent advances in robotic precision and learning-based control, existing autonomous navigation methods remain limited by their reliance on static reward functions and the lack of explicit procedural reasoning regarding anatomical context and task progression. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a vision-language procedural reasoning (VL-PR) framework for autonomous guidewire navigation. The framework integrates a multimodal large language model (MLLM) as a procedural reasoning module that interprets real-time visual observations to infer high-level navigation contexts. Instead of generating low-level control commands, the inferred procedural insights enable context-aware reward adaptation by dynamically adjusting the importance of reward components across different navigation phases. This approach allows a single policy to resolve competing objectives and handle complex transitions while preserving a consistent global task goal. Experiments on a physical robotic platform across diverse vascular scenarios demonstrate enhanced task reliability and streamlined navigational efficiency, highlighting the advantages over static-reward methods and offering a scalable solution for complex and multi-task robotic endovascular procedures.

</details>


### [208] [From Grasps to Dexterity: Large-Scale Grasp Pretraining for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30749)
*Ying Yuan,Xinyu Liu,Sriram Krishna,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探索利用大规模灵巧抓取数据集预训练机器人进行关节工具使用操作。通过层次化模仿学习框架，结合高层子目标预测和低层目标条件控制器，在仿真和真实世界实验中显著提升了工具操作任务的成功率，证明了抓取数据可作为接触密集型灵巧操作的预训练资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模灵巧抓取数据集编码了丰富的手-物体交互先验知识，但其应用主要局限于抓取生成和放置操作。研究者希望探索这些数据是否能够支持关节工具使用中的功能性灵巧操作，即机器人需要获取工具、保持接触并操作其功能活动部件。

Method: 采用层次化模仿学习框架，结合高层手部子目标预测与低层目标条件控制器。从大规模灵巧抓取注释中构建355k轨迹抓取预训练数据集，用于预训练低层控制器，然后在下游任务演示上进行微调。引入DexCraft仿真基准，包含6个需要协调手指运动的关节工具使用任务。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，该方法优于端到端扩散策略基线和从零训练的层次化策略。在真实世界中，相比DP3，完整任务成功率提升了33.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 抓取数据集不仅可以作为抓取合成的资源，还可以作为接触密集型灵巧操作的可扩展预训练数据。这为机器人灵巧操作提供了新的数据利用途径，证明了预训练策略在复杂工具使用任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Large-scale dexterous grasp datasets encode rich priors over hand-object interaction, but their use has largely been confined to grasp generation and pick-and-place manipulation. We study whether such data can instead support functional dexterity in articulated tool use, where a robot must acquire a tool, maintain contact, and operate its functional moving parts. We adapt a hierarchical imitation learning framework that combines high-level hand sub-goal prediction with a low-level goal-conditioned controller. We construct a 355k-trajectory grasp-pretraining dataset from large-scale dexterous grasp annotations and use it to pretrain the low-level controller. The controller is then fine-tuned on downstream task demonstrations. To evaluate this setting, we introduce DexCraft, a simulation benchmark with six articulated tool-use tasks requiring coordinated finger motion. Across simulation and real-world experiments, our approach outperforms end-to-end diffusion policy baselines and hierarchical policies trained from scratch. In the real world, it improves full-task success by 33.3 percentage points over DP3. These results show that grasp datasets can serve not only as resources for grasp synthesis, but also as scalable pretraining data for contact-rich dexterous manipulation. Videos are shown on https://yingyuan0414.github.io/grasp2dexterity/ .

</details>


### [209] [Wind and State Estimation on SE(3): Comparative Evaluation of EKF and UKF with Continuous and Discrete Quadrotor Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30804)
*Hiranya Udagedara,Adam Bigsby,Mahdis Bisheban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出基于SE(3)离散动力学模型结合UKF的四旋翼风速估计方法，通过对比离散/连续模型与EKF/UKF滤波器，在仿真和实飞中验证了离散模型+UKF在低成本传感器下仍可实现高精度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼无人机因机动性、小型化和低成本优势被广泛用于风速估计，但其强非线性特性导致传统模型基方法存在精度瓶颈。现有研究多采用连续或离散化动力学模型，但离散化过程引入的近似误差可能影响估计性能。

Method: 1. 构建SE(3)流形上的离散动力学模型：采用基于离散拉格朗日理论的Lie Group Variational Integrator，避免传统离散化的近似误差；
2. 设计双滤波器架构：分别将离散/连续动力学模型与EKF、UKF结合进行风速估计；
3. 多场景验证：在MATLAB中开展悬停/轨迹跟踪仿真测试，并进行真实环境下的户外飞行实验。

Result: 数值仿真和实飞结果表明：离散SE(3)动力学+UKF组合在悬停和轨迹跟踪任务中均优于其他配置，尤其在低成本传感器条件下仍能保持高估计精度，同时有效维持飞行轨迹跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 离散四旋翼模型与UKF的协同作用可显著提升风速估计精度，该方法不仅适用于风速估计，还可扩展至其他依赖低成本传感器的高精度任务，为无人机自主导航提供新范式。

Abstract: Use of quadrotor UAVs for wind velocity estimation is gaining popularity in recent studies, leveraging their maneuverability, compact size and low cost. Among available approaches, model-based wind velocity estimation is most commonly used, since it relies only on onboard sensors. However, as the quadrotor is a highly nonlinear system, thus making this task challenging. This study evaluate the use of both discrete and continuous dynamic equations of the quadrotor UAV for wind velocity estimation on SE(3), rather than commonly adapted continuous or discretized form. Lie Group Variational Integrator, developed on discrete Lagrangian is used as the discrete model without any approximation or discritization. The study assess both the discrete and continuous form of the quadrotor dynamics on SE(3) using Extended Kalman filter (EKF), and Unscented Kalman filter (UKF). The quadrotor UAV performance is evaluated in both MATLAB-based numerical simulations and free outdoor flight. The numerical simulations are conducted during both hovering and trajectory-tracking flights. Results demonstrate that, by using discrete SE(3) dynamics coupled with UKF, the quadrotor achieves higher estimation accuracy while maintaining trajectory tracking, even with low-cost sensors. These findings highlight the potential of discrete quadrotor models with UKF not only for wind velocity estimation but also for other high-accuracy tasks, even when relying on low-cost onboard sensors.

</details>


### [210] [Off the Rails: Hijacking the Scoring Head in Generative End-to-End Driving Planners with Safety-Violating Adversarial Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30807)
*Halima Bouzidi,Mboutidem Ekemini Mkpong,Haoyu Liu,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出Derail对抗框架，针对端到端自动驾驶中生成式规划器的评分头攻击面进行攻击，导致轨迹选择从安全变为不安全，得分下降39-80%，碰撞率高达50%，显著优于其他攻击方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式自动驾驶规划器依赖固定候选轨迹的评分头进行决策，其决策边界较小，成为潜在攻击目标。研究旨在揭示这一设计缺陷并验证其可被利用性。

Method: Derail框架通过针对性攻击评分头，在多种生成式规划器上实现轨迹选择翻转，对比通用损失最大化和特征发散攻击评估效果。

Result: 攻击导致得分下降39-80%，碰撞率达50%，显著优于其他方法；分析表明违反安全目标对攻击有效性至关重要，评分头推理模式是重复出现的攻击面。

Conclusion: 评分头的推理模式是生成式自动驾驶规划器的关键脆弱点，需明确防御考量；安全违反型目标对攻击有效性具有决定性作用。

Abstract: Generative models have recently seen rapid adoption in End-to-End (E2E) autonomous driving (AD), with diffusion-based denoising and vocabulary-based retrieval becoming the dominant trajectory-decoding paradigms. Despite their architectural diversity, current generative AD planners share a common inference pattern: a fixed set of candidate trajectories (anchors, vocabulary entries, or proposal queries) is scored by one or more learned heads conditioned on the Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) features, and the highest-scored candidate is returned as the final trajectory. Under this design, the scoring head is the only barrier between perception and the motion command, and its decision margins between competing candidates are often small. We introduce \textsc{Derail}, an adversarial framework that exploits this scoring-head attack surface. Evaluated on various generative planners, \textsc{Derail} flips the trajectory selection from a safe to an unsafe candidate, with score drops of $39$--$80\%$ and collision rates of up to $50\%$, consistently outperforming generic loss-maximization and feature-divergence attacks. Our analysis suggests that safety-violating objectives govern attack effectiveness against generative AD planners, and that the scoring-head inference pattern itself is a recurring attack surface worth explicit defensive consideration.

</details>


### [211] [TAPE: Tether-Aware Path Planning for Autonomous Exploration of Unknown 3D Cavities Using a Tangle-Compatible Tethered Aerial Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30817)
*Louis Petit,Alexis Lussier Desbiens*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个三维未知洞穴自主探索方法TAPE，通过分层架构同时优化行进距离与缆绳长度，在保持缆绳安全的前提下仅增加4.1%路径距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对三维未知洞穴探索中缆绳缠绕风险与行进效率的矛盾，现有方法难以同时优化行进距离和缆绳展开长度，且缆绳缠绕主要受局部路径影响而非全局规划。

Method: 采用两级分层架构：全局层基于前沿点求解TSP最小化总距离；局部层通过可调决策函数动态平衡路径成本与缆绳长度，参数调节实现双目标权衡。

Result: 仿真与实地测试显示：相比纯TSP方案，TAPE平均增加4.1%行进距离，但将缆绳超限率从53%降至0%，实现100%缆绳安全约束满足。

Conclusion: TAPE作为首个联合优化行进距离与缆绳长度的三维洞穴探索方法，在可接受路径代价下显著提升缆绳安全性，为受限环境机器人探索提供新范式。

Abstract: This letter presents the first method for autonomous exploration of unknown cavities in three dimensions (3D) that focuses on minimizing the distance traveled and the length of tether unwound. Considering that the tether entanglements are little influenced by the global path, our approach employs a 2-level hierarchical architecture. The global frontier-based planning solves a Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) to minimize the distance. The local planning attempts to minimize the path cost and the tether length using an adjustable decision function whose parameters play on the trade-off between these two values. The proposed method, TAPE, is evaluated through detailed simulation studies as well as field tests. On average, our method generates a 4.1% increase in distance traveled compared to the TSP solution without our local planner, with which the length of the tether remains below the maximum allowed value in 53% of the simulated cases against 100% with our method.

</details>


### [212] [Robustness-Based Synthesis for Time Window Temporal Logic Specifications via Mixed-Integer Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30820)
*Philip Smith,Ahmad Ahmad,Kevin Leahy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于混合整数线性规划（MILP）的控制合成方法，将时间窗口时序逻辑（TWTL）任务规格转化为鲁棒性优化的MILP问题，支持开环控制序列优化和闭环模型预测控制（MPC），其中闭环方法通过任务自适应预测时域显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对离散时间线性系统在TWTL任务规格下的控制输入合成问题，现有方法可能缺乏对时序约束的紧凑表达能力或鲁棒性优化机制，需结合TWTL的定量语义提升控制策略的可靠性和效率。

Method: 将TWTL公式的鲁棒满足性编码为混合整数线性约束，构建MILP问题以最大化鲁棒度；提出两种合成方案：1）开环优化全控制序列；2）闭环MPC采用任务自适应预测时域，利用TWTL的确定性有限自动机（DFA）动态确定当前子任务，缩短预测窗口。

Result: 证明任何正目标值的可行解均保证布尔满足；闭环MPC通过任务自适应时域显著减少每次求解的计算量，相比开环方法更高效，适用于实时控制场景。

Conclusion: 该方法有效将TWTL任务转化为可优化的MILP问题，通过鲁棒性最大化提升控制策略可靠性，任务自适应MPC框架在保持性能的同时大幅降低计算开销，为时序约束系统的实时控制提供了新途径。

Abstract: Time Window Temporal Logic (TWTL) is a rich specification language for cyber-physical systems that can compactly express sequential tasks with explicit timing constraints. In this paper, we consider the problem of synthesizing control inputs for discrete-time linear systems subject to TWTL task specifications. Building on the quantitative semantics (robustness) recently introduced for TWTL in [1], we encode the robust satisfaction of a TWTL formula as a set of Mixed-Integer Linear constraints and pose synthesis as a Mixed Integer Linear Program (MILP) that maximizes the robustness degree. We prove that any feasible solution with positive objective value guarantees Boolean satisfaction of the specification. We address two synthesis settings: an \emph{open-loop} formulation that optimizes the full control sequence from the initial state, and a \emph{closed-loop} receding-horizon Model Predictive Controller (MPC) formulation that re-solves the MILP at each step using the current measured state. A key feature of our MPC formulation is a \emph{task-adaptive horizon} that exploits the TWTL Deterministic Finite Automaton (DFA) to determine the active sub-task at each step, limiting the prediction horizon to the remaining window of the current task rather than the full formula horizon, this makes each re-solve significantly cheaper than the initial open-loop solve.

</details>


### [213] [Sampling-Based Coordination-Informed Multi-Objective Multi-Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30893)
*Antonio Marino,Esteban Restrepo,Soon-jo Chung,Paolo Robuffo Giordano,Claudio Pacchierotti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CIMORL框架，通过分布式权重预测、特权专家训练和帕累托最优理论保证，实现多机器人系统的高效协调优化，在实验和真实场景中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖固定或集中式协调，限制了多智能体系统的适应性和分布式部署能力，无法满足动态竞争目标的优化需求。

Method: 设计CIMORL框架，包含：1）分布式权重预测机制实现目标动态平衡；2）特权专家训练策略利用全局信息指导去中心化部署；3）提供帕累托最优解理论保障。衍生CIMORL-TS（树搜索）和CIMORL-MPPI（模型预测路径积分）两种采样变体。

Result: 在合作与对抗场景中，相比基线方法超体积提升21.2%，策略稳定性显著增强；Crazyflie无人机实验验证了在部分可观测环境下的资源分配与攻防场景鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CIMORL框架有效突破传统协调瓶颈，通过理论-实验双重验证，为分布式多目标优化提供了可扩展解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems must simultaneously optimize competing objectives while maintaining coordinated behavior. Existing multi-agent reinforcement learning approaches often rely on fixed or centralized coordination, which limits adaptability and violates distributed constraints. This work introduces the Coordination-Informed Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning (CIMORL) framework, integrating a distributed weight prediction mechanism, a privileged expert training strategy, and theoretical guarantees for Pareto-optimal solutions. We present the base CIMORL method alongside two sampling-based variants, CIMORL-TS (Tree Search) and CIMORL-MPPI (MPPI), which leverage privileged global information during training to enable fully decentralized deployment. Experimental validation in cooperative and adversarial scenarios demonstrates a $21.2\%$ hypervolume improvement and superior policy stability compared to state-of-the-art baselines. Real-world experiments with Crazyflie drones further validate the framework's robustness in resource allocation and multi-attacker multi-defend scenarios under partial observability.

</details>


### [214] [The Quadruped Soft Tail: Compliant Grasping and Swabbing for Contamination Surveys in Harsh Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30900)
*Harald Minde Hansen,Nandita Gallacher,Kristin Y. Pettersen,Jan Tommy Gravdahl,Mario di Castro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一种带有软尾的四足机器人系统，用于放射性区域铍污染调查，结合腿式移动与柔性操作，避免人员暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射性区域电缆和电子设备杂乱，传统刚性机械臂难以操作，需安全完成铍污染采样任务。

Method: 设计轻量化软质肌腱驱动尾，含柔性脊柱和抓取器；开发闭式运动学模型及奇异鲁棒任务空间控制器实现遥操作。

Result: 抓取器动作对机器人形态影响小，共模肌腱驱动有效调节刚度；运动学模型支持实时控制，实验验证系统可行性。

Conclusion: 系统融合腿式敏捷性与柔性操作，适用于CERN铍污染调查及类似危险环境，为复杂场景提供通用解决方案。

Abstract: Beryllium contamination surveys in radioactive areas are challenging for robots in environments cluttered with cables and electronics. To address this problem, we have developed a novel quadruped system augmentation: A lightweight, soft, and compliant tendon-actuated robotic tail mounted on a quadruped robot. The tail features a hollow, flexible backbone and a tendon-actuated soft gripper that enables the robot to pick up sampling tissues, swab contaminated surfaces, and release the tissues at designated collection locations for subsequent beryllium analysis. To enable intuitive teleoperation, a closed-form kinematic model and a singularity-robust task-space controller are developed. Experimental results demonstrate that gripper actuation has a negligible effect on robot shape, while common-mode tendon actuation provides an effective mechanism for stiffness modulation and preload control. Furthermore, experimental validation indicates that the proposed kinematic model provides a suitable basis for real-time task-space control. The proposed system combines the agility of legged locomotion with the compliance of soft robotic manipulation, enabling the complete contamination-survey procedure to be performed without human exposure. While motivated by beryllium contamination surveys at CERN, the proposed quadruped soft-tail concept is broadly applicable to legged robots operating in cluttered, confined, or hazardous environments where conventional rigid-link manipulators are undesirable.

</details>


### [215] [Motion Planning in Compressed Representation Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30940)
*Lukas Lao Beyer,Sertac Karaman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种结合深度学习与基于模型规划的运动规划框架，通过高压缩比自动编码器和层次化离散标记潜空间搜索，实现灵活高效的目标导向行为生成，在nuPlan和Waymo数据集上验证了其在闭环规划和多智能体场景合成中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习方法虽能捕捉复杂行为，但基于搜索/优化的规划算法因灵活性和可融入领域知识而不可或缺。现有方法难以统一两种范式的优势。

Method: 构建高压缩比自动编码器，其潜空间由层次化离散标记组成；在潜空间中直接执行搜索，通过优化测试时指定的目标函数生成运动轨迹。

Result: 在nuPlan和Waymo开放运动数据集上，该方法在闭环运动规划和多智能体引导场景合成任务中表现优异，无需任务特定训练即可适应多种引导行为生成需求。

Conclusion: 提出的生成框架成功统一了数据驱动与模型驱动规划方法，通过潜空间搜索实现了高效、灵活且逼真的运动规划解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning methods have vastly expanded the capabilities of motion planning in robotics applications, as learning priors from large-scale data has been shown to be essential in capturing the highly complex behavior required for solving tasks such as manipulation or navigation for autonomous vehicles. At the same time, model-based planning algorithms based on search or optimization remain an essential tool due to their flexibility, efficiency, and the ability to incorporate domain knowledge via expert-designed algorithms and objective functions. We propose a new generative framework to unify these two paradigms. First, we learn an autoencoder with a high compression ratio and a latent space of hierarchically ordered, discrete-valued tokens. Leveraging both the dimensionality reduction and the hierarchical coarse-to-fine structure learned by this autoencoder, we then perform motion planning by directly searching in the latent space of tokens. This search can optimize arbitrary objective functions specified at test time, providing a large degree of flexibility while maintaining efficiency and producing realistic solutions by relying on the generative capabilities of the highly compressed autoencoder. We evaluate our method on nuPlan and the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, showing how latent space search can be used for a variety of guided behavior generation tasks, achieving strong performance for closed-loop motion planning and multi-agent guided scenario synthesis without requiring any task-specific training.

</details>


### [216] [Multisensory Continual Learning: Adapting Pretrained Visuomotor Policies to Force](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30988)
*Jaden Clark,Changhao Wang,Yihuai Gao,Seongheon Hong,Hojung Choi,Mark Cutkosky,Yifan Hou,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MuSe通过多阶段融合、多感官未来预测及经验回放，将有限多感官数据融入预训练视觉策略，在接触密集任务中提升性能同时保持原任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作依赖视觉外感官反馈（如力、触觉），但多感官数据集稀缺且硬件/任务特定，难以预训练所有传感器策略，需持续学习适应新模态而不损失原有性能。

Method: 提出MuSe框架，结合多阶段融合、多感官未来预测及预训练数据经验回放，将力-扭矩传感集成到预训练纯视觉策略中。

Result: 在真实世界接触密集任务中，MuSe显著优于基线方法，同时保持或提升原预训练任务性能，证明多感官数据可泛化至微调分布外场景。

Conclusion: 适度规模的多感官数据集能有效增强通用机器人能力，MuSe为跨模态持续学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Robot manipulation often relies on sensory feedback beyond vision, particularly in contact-rich settings where force, tactile, or audio signals reveal interaction states that are not directly observable from images. However, these modalities are often hardware- and task-specific, and large-scale multisensory robot datasets remain scarce. As a result, it is impractical to pretrain policies with every sensor they may encounter. We study multisensory continual learning: adapting a pretrained robot policy to new tasks with newly introduced modalities while preserving performance under the original sensor suite. We propose MuSe, which incorporates limited multisensory data into pretrained vision-only policies through multi-stage fusion, multisensory future prediction, and experience replay over pretraining data. We instantiate MuSe by augmenting a pretrained vision-only policy with force-torque sensing and evaluate it on real-world manipulation tasks. Our experiments show that MuSe performs strongly on contact-rich finetuning tasks while preserving, and in some cases improving, performance on the original pretraining tasks. These results suggest that a modest multisensory dataset can improve general robot capabilities beyond the finetuning distribution. Project website: https://jadenvc.github.io/multisensory-continual-learning/

</details>


### [217] [Ground Plane-Aided Extrinsic Calibration of Inertial and RGB-D Sensors for Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31019)
*Ilyar Asl Sabbaghian Hokmabadi,Mahdis Bisheban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需专用设备的无人机IMU与RGB-D相机外参校准方法，通过深度学习地面分割提取地面点法向量，结合重力向量实现鲁棒估计，性能优于MATLAB工具箱且接近Kalibr，无需标定板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机传感器外参校准方法依赖专用设备、平面目标及初始参数估计，限制了实际应用场景的便捷性和普适性。

Method: 基于深度学习的RGB-D图像地面分割技术提取地面点云，计算其法向量，结合加速度计测量重力向量方向，在已知地面法向与重力方向关系的前提下，通过鲁棒优化算法求解外参矩阵。

Result: 实验表明该方法精度显著优于MATLAB工具箱，与主流工具Kalibr性能相当，且无需使用棋盘格等专用标定目标。

Conclusion: 该方法通过环境感知替代传统标定物，实现了免设备、免预配置的在线外参校准，为无人机自主导航系统提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate extrinsic calibration of inertial sensors, such as Inertial Measurement Units (IMUs) and cameras is crucial for trajectory estimation of Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs). While numerous calibration methods have been proposed, these techniques often rely on specialized equipment, planar targets, and an initial estimate of the calibration parameters.
  In this research, we propose a targetless calibration method designed for UAVs equipped with IMUs and RGB-Depth (RGB-D) cameras. Our approach leverages deep-learning-based floor-segmentation to extract ground points from the depth channel of RGB-D images. Subsequently, the normal vector to these points is estimated. The known orientation of the normal to the floor segment and the gravity vector sensed in the accelerometer's frame are utilized in a robust estimation approach to estimate the extrinsic calibration parameters. We illustrate that the developed method outperforms MATLAB's Toolboxes and exhibits similar performance to Kalibr without the use of specialized checkerboard targets.

</details>


### [218] [Labimus: A Simulation and Benchmark for Humanoid Dexterous Manipulation in Chemical Laboratory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31037)
*Yuhan Wu,Zhao Jin,Tao Li,Yuheng Zhang,Zhengping Che,Jian Tang,Zhichao Wang,Shuo Wang,Jun Jiang,Xiaobo Li,Yanyong Zhang,Yan Xia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Labimus是首个针对有机化学实验室的人形灵巧操作基准，通过真实到仿真建模重建30多个实验资产，整合仪器、粉末物理和闭环读数，定义原子操作和称量流程，揭示任务完成与实验精度间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实验室自动化难以标准化精度关键的动态操作（如固-固转移），且缺乏评估人形机器人实验室操作的基准，亟需解决实验精度与任务完成脱节问题。

Method: 构建Labimus基准：①基于真实工作站建模30+高保真资产；②集成关节仪器、粒子粉末物理及闭环仪器读数；③定义6项原子操作和7步固体称量流程；④设计精度感知评估协议，综合测量任务完成、实验精度和长时执行。

Result: 三种策略在标准布局和扰动环境下测试显示：成功完成任务的策略仍无法满足实验协议要求的定量容差，暴露任务完成与实验有效性的根本性脱节。

Conclusion: Labimus为开发可靠实验室人形机器人提供新测试平台，推动解决精度关键操作中的动态适应与标准化难题，填补实验室操作评估空白。

Abstract: Laboratory automation has made remarkable progress through robotic platforms and AI-driven scientific reasoning. However, many laboratory operations (e.g., solid--solid transfer) remain inherently dynamic and require real-time adaptation to different materials and experimental conditions. Such precision-critical manipulations are difficult to standardize, motivating the use of humanoid robots with dexterous hands. Despite this opportunity, no existing benchmark evaluates humanoid manipulation in precision-critical laboratory environments. We present Labimus, to our knowledge, the first benchmark for humanoid dexterous manipulation in organic chemistry laboratories. Labimus reconstructs over 30 functionally faithful assets from real organic chemistry workstations through real-to-sim modeling, collectively covering the core operations of routine organic chemistry experiments. The benchmark integrates articulated laboratory instruments, particle-based powder physics, and closed-loop instrument readouts, enabling a complete manipulation-to-measurement pipeline. It further defines six atomic operations and a seven-step solid-weighing workflow derived from real laboratory standard operating procedures. We introduce a precision-aware evaluation protocol designed to jointly measure task completion, experimental precision, and long-horizon execution. We benchmark three representative policies under procedural layouts and environmental perturbations. Results reveal a precision gap: policies that successfully complete laboratory tasks can still fail to satisfy the quantitative tolerances required by experimental protocols. Our benchmark exposes a fundamental disconnect between task completion and experimental validity, providing a new testbed for developing reliable humanoid robots for scientific laboratories.

</details>


### [219] [Efficient Sim-to-Real Transfer of World-Action Models from Synthetic Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31101)
*Zixing Wang,Kausik Sivakumar,Jinghuan Shang,Yafei Hu,Zhaoming Xie,Ran Gong,Xiaohan Zhang,Karl Schmeckpeper*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究首次实现世界-动作模型从模拟到真实机器人操作的零样本迁移，通过合成数据训练视频扩散模型并部署于Franka机器人，在三项任务中取得35%平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器人操作中模拟到现实的迁移难题，验证世界-动作模型能否仅用合成数据训练并直接部署到真实环境，避免依赖昂贵的真实机器人演示。

Method: 基于Cosmos Policy视频扩散模型构建视觉运动控制策略，采用广泛域随机化的模拟环境生成约800组合成演示数据，通过AnyTask运动规划管道进行训练，最终零样本部署至Franka机器人。

Result: 在物体提升、抽屉开启和拾取放置三项任务中实现35%平均成功率，无真实机器人演示数据参与训练，验证了模型从模拟到现实的直接迁移能力。

Conclusion: 首次证明世界-动作模型可成功实现机器人操作的sim-to-real零样本迁移，为低成本部署学习策略提供新路径。

Abstract: Bridging the sim-to-real gap is a core challenge in deploying learned manipulation policies. Sim-to-real learning is attractive because it can replace expensive real robot demonstrations with scalable synthetic data, yet world-action models have not previously been shown to transfer from simulation to real robotic manipulation. We study whether a world-action model can be trained from synthetic priors and deployed zero-shot in the real world. To this end, we build upon Cosmos Policy, a video diffusion model adapted for visuomotor control. We construct simulation environments with extensive domain randomization and generate demonstrations using the AnyTask motion planning pipeline. We evaluate our approach across object lifting, drawer opening, and pick-and-place tasks using ${\sim}800$ synthetic demonstrations per task and no real demonstrations. When deployed zero-shot on a Franka Robot, our policy attains a 35\% average success rate. To our knowledge, this represents the first successful sim-to-real transfer of a world-action model for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [220] [What Probing Reveals about Autonomous Driving: Linking Internal Prediction Errors to Ego Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31106)
*Hyeonchang Jeon,Kyungbeom Kim,Eugene Vinitsky,Kyung-Joong Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过线性探测和因果干预技术，揭示了自动驾驶策略在预测周围车辆运动和生成安全轨迹方面的局限性，发现即使闭环性能良好，策略在近碰撞事件中仍缺乏及时预测能力，而纠正错误预测可显著改善规划安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶策略虽在大规模数据集和快速模拟器训练下表现出良好的闭环性能，但摘要评分无法揭示策略是否真正具备预测周围车辆运动和生成安全轨迹的能力，还是仅依赖脆弱的启发式方法。研究旨在深入理解这些策略的内在局限性和弱点。

Method: 研究采用线性探测(linear probing)和目标扰动(targeted perturbations)技术，在数据驱动的行为克隆和模拟驱动的强化学习模型中评估预测和规划能力。通过研究这些能力随训练规模的变化，追踪内部信号的涌现、停滞或失效。同时使用因果干预(causal intervention)验证预测准确性对规划质量的影响。

Result: 尽管策略在闭环测试中表现良好，但在近碰撞事件中往往无法及时形成对周围车辆的准确预测，揭示了自我规划中可用预测信号的局限性。因果干预实验表明，纠正错误预测能够显著改善自我规划，使其生成更安全的轨迹。

Conclusion: 自动驾驶策略的闭环性能并不能完全反映其内在的预测和规划能力。策略在近碰撞场景中的预测能力不足是一个关键限制因素，而提升预测准确性是改善规划安全性的有效途径。这为未来自动驾驶策略的开发提供了重要的改进方向。

Abstract: Large-scale datasets and fast simulators have enabled improvements in driving policies that appear safe and robust, yet strong performance in nominal scenarios can still mask flawed reasoning and unsafe heuristics.
