Abstract: Many path planning algorithms have been introduced so far, but most are costly, in path cost and in processing time, in large-scale uncluttered 3D environments such as underground mining stopes explored by an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV). Rapidly-exploring Random Tree (RRT) algorithms are popular because of their probabilistic completeness and rapidity in finding a feasible path in single-query problems. Many of the algorithms (e.g. Informed RRT*, RRT#) developed to improve RRT need considerable time to converge in large environments. Shortcutting an RRT is an old idea that has been proven to outperform RRT variants. This paper introduces a new method, RRT-Rope, that aims at finding a near-optimal solution in a drastically shorter amount of time. The proposed approach benefits from fast computation of a feasible path with an altered version of RRT-connect, and post-processes it quickly with a deterministic shortcutting technique, taking advantage of intermediate nodes added to each branch of the tree. This paper presents simulations and statistics carried out to show the efficiency of RRT-Rope, which gives better results in terms of path cost and computation time than other popular RRT variations and shortening techniques in all our simulation environments, and is up to 70% faster than the next best algorithm in a representative stope.

</details>


### [257] [Adapting Generalist Robot Policies with Semantic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31958)
*Jagdeep Singh Bhatia,Andrew Wagenmaker,William Chen,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SARL方法，通过优化语言提示空间而非直接调整动作分布，使通用机器人策略能高效适应复杂长程任务，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL方法依赖基础策略动作分布与高性能策略相近，在复杂/长任务中失效；需新范式突破预训练分布限制。

Method: SARL将通用策略视为可控技能先验，通过在线交互优化语言提示空间，利用预训练技能组合实现语义化探索与任务求解。

Result: 在真实场景与仿真基准中验证：SARL使VLA模型获得全新能力，显著优于现有机器人行为优化方法。

Conclusion: 语言提示空间优化为机器人适应复杂任务提供高效路径，突破传统动作空间RL局限，推动通用策略部署应用。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies learn a diverse repertoire of behaviors from large-scale pretraining. In principle, this makes them excellent priors for downstream adaptation via reinforcement learning (RL). In practice, however, standard RL methods leveraging this prior optimize directly over robot actions, requiring the base policy's action distribution to be close to that of a performant policy from the start. This assumption breaks down for complex or long-horizon tasks that fall outside the pretraining distribution. Our key insight is that, for sufficiently expressive generalist policies, language prompts are an effective alternative space for learning to solve such tasks: modulating language inputs elicits skills already within the policy's repertoire, which can be composed to solve tasks beyond its zero-shot capabilities. We propose Semantic Action Reinforcement Learning (SARL), which learns to optimize this prompt space through online interaction, treating the generalist policy as a controllable skill prior. Importantly, leveraging pretrained skills rather than learning new ones from scratch yields structured, semantically meaningful exploration and highly efficient online improvement, and learning to modulate prompts through experience grounds them in induced real-world behaviors for robust task-solving. Across real-world settings and simulated benchmarks, we show SARL unlocks fundamentally new capabilities -- adapting VLA behavior to solve complex, long-horizon tasks -- and significantly outperforms existing approaches for improving robot behavior in deployment.

</details>


### [258] [OopsieVerse: A Safety Benchmark with Damage-Aware Simulation for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31993)
*Arnav Balaji,Arpit Bahety,Sriniket Ambatipudi,Daniel Lam,Junhong Xu,Roberto Martín-Martín*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出OOPSIEVERSE，一个用于感知损坏的家庭操作的统一仿真框架和基准。该框架解决现有仿真器缺乏损坏检测机制的问题，通过DAMAGESIM框架将物理交互转换为损坏信号，并在多个仿真器中验证其通用性，支持安全机器人操作研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作能力快速发展，但物理安全仍是家庭机器人部署的主要障碍。任务成功不足以保证安全性，机器人可能损坏自身或周围环境。仿真虽提供安全训练环境，但现有仿真器缺乏检测、量化和表示损坏的通用机制。

Method: 引入OOPSIEVERSE统一仿真框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)DAMAGESIM-与仿真器无关的损坏检测和量化框架，将接触力、温度变化、液体相互作用等转换为机械、热或流体损坏信号；(2)家庭任务套件，评估常见损坏模式并区分任务完成与安全执行。在OmniGibson和RoboCasa两个不同物理后端的仿真器中实例化验证。

Result: 展示了框架的通用性和实用性，包括：(1)通过实时损坏反馈指导更安全的演示收集；(2)通过损坏条件模仿学习和强化学习训练更安全的操作策略；(3)基准测试最先进视觉语言动作策略的安全性；(4)改善仿真到现实转移策略的实际安全性。

Conclusion: OOPSIEVERSE作为开源基础，为系统性、可扩展的安全机器人操作研究提供支撑。结果表明该框架在多个应用场景中有效，为安全机器人部署奠定基础。

Abstract: While robotic manipulation capabilities have advanced rapidly, physical safety remains a major barrier to deploying household robots: task success is insufficient if the robot damages itself or its surroundings. Simulation offers a harm-free alternative to costly and dangerous real-world training and evaluation, yet existing simulators lack general mechanisms to detect, quantify, and represent damage. To address this gap, we introduce OOPSIEVERSE, a unified simulation framework and benchmark for damage-aware household manipulation. OOPSIEVERSE provides damage as an explicit, physically-grounded, and taskagnostic signal by converting sources such as contact forces, temperature changes, and liquid interactions into corresponding mechanical, thermal or fluid damage. OOPSIEVERSE comprises two core elements: (1) DAMAGESIM, a simulator-agnostic framework for detecting and quantifying damage during navigation and manipulation, and (2) a suite of household tasks designed to evaluate common damage modes and distinguish between task completion and safe execution. We demonstrate the generality of our framework by instantiating DAMAGESIM in two simulators with different physics backends, OmniGibson (Nvidia Omniverse) and RoboCasa (MuJoCo). We further showcase the utility of OOPSIEVERSE across multiple use cases, including (1) guiding safer demonstration collection via real-time damage feedback, (2) learning safer manipulation policies through damage-conditioned imitation learning and reinforcement learning, (3) benchmarking the safety of state-of-the-art Vision Language Action policies, and (4) improving real-world safety of sim-to-real transferred policies. Together, our results highlight the potential of OOPSIEVERSE as an open-source foundation for systematic, scalable research on safe robot manipulation. For code and more information, please refer to https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/oopsieverse/

</details>


### [259] [Human-as-Humanoid: Enabling Zero-Shot Humanoid Learning from Ego-Exo Human Videos with Human-Aligned Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32009)
*Xiaopeng Lin,Ruoqi Yang,Shijie Lian,Zhaolong Shen,Bin Yu,Changti Wu,Haibao Liu,Yuxiang Zhang,Hong Li,Qiyuan Su,Haochen Liu,Xuguo He,Yukun Shi,Cong Huang,Zhirui Zhang,Bojun Cheng,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Human-as-Humanoid框架，通过同步ego-exo视频、分阶段逆向运动学（IK）与正向运动学（FK）监督，将人类演示数据高效转化为高自由度人形机器人的可执行动作监督，显著提升数据吞吐量并实现真实机器人部署泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型需高质量观察-动作监督数据，但高自由度人形机器人数据扩展困难；遥操作数据有限，人类第一视角视频缺乏可直接执行的机器人动作。

Method: 基于60-DoF上半身人形机器人PrimeU，利用同步ego-exo视频配对部署对齐的第一视角观测与外视角运动恢复，通过分阶段IK将人类动作重定向为控制器对齐的60-DoF动作块，并采用FK感知监督训练VLA模型以保留手腕和指尖任务空间几何结构。

Result: 实验验证运动恢复、机器人动作空间和真实机器人部署三个层面；数据吞吐量较遥操作提升4.8-7.2倍；仅用转换后的人类标签微调的策略在多个下游任务中实现真实机器人部署泛化，无需目标任务机器人演示。

Conclusion: Human-as-Humanoid框架有效解决高自由度人形机器人VLA训练的数据瓶颈，实现人类演示到可执行监督的高效转换，显著提升数据利用效率并支持零样本真实部署。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models across robot embodiments require high-quality observation--action supervision to learn deployable action distributions, yet scaling such robot data remains difficult, especially for high-DoF humanoids. Teleoperation provides controller-aligned supervision, while human egocentric videos capture diverse bimanual manipulation but do not directly provide executable robot actions. We introduce Human-as-Humanoid, a human-to-humanoid supervision framework that enables near-real-time human-centric action generation, making human demonstrations usable for high-DoF humanoid VLA training by jointly aligning the robot embodiment, the sensing setup, and the action-label interface. Built on PrimeU, a human-aligned 60-DoF upper-body humanoid, Human-as-Humanoid uses synchronized ego-exo videos to pair deployment-aligned egocentric observations with exocentric motion recovery, retargets the recovered human motion through staged Inverse Kinematics (IK) into controller-aligned 60-DoF action chunks, and trains the VLA model with Forward Kinematics (FK)-aware supervision to preserve wrist and fingertip task-space geometry. This converts large-scale human demonstrations from visual observations into executable observation--action supervision for the target humanoid. Experiments validate the conversion chain at the motion-recovery, robot-action-space, and real-robot deployment levels. Human-as-Humanoid yields a 4.8--7.2x raw demonstration-throughput gain over humanoid teleoperation in our data-collection analysis, and on several downstream tasks, policies post-trained only with the converted human labels generalize to real-robot deployment without target-task robot demonstrations. The official project website is available at https://zgc-embodyai.github.io/Human-as-Humanoid.

</details>


### [260] [Freeform Preference Learning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32027)
*Marcel Torne,Anubha Mahajan,Abhijnya Bhat,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Freeform Preference Learning (FPL)方法，通过自然语言定义的多维度人类偏好训练机器人策略，在6项长期操作任务中较传统方法提升38个百分点，并实现无需重训练的行为动态调整能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略优化受限于稀疏成功标签和二元偏好信号的模糊性，难以捕捉复杂任务中的多维质量维度。

Method: FPL允许标注者定义自然语言偏好轴（如速度/安全性），收集各轴成对偏好数据，训练语言条件奖励模型生成轴特定奖励，进而训练奖励条件策略实现多维优化。

Result: 在4个真实世界和2个模拟长期操作任务中，FPL较稀疏奖励和二元偏好方法提升38个百分点；同时实现无显式子任务分割的密集进度信号学习、数据未见的行为组合性，以及测试时动态行为调整能力。

Conclusion: FPL突破了传统奖励设计瓶颈，为机器人策略学习提供了可扩展的多维人类反馈框架，显著提升了复杂任务性能与交互灵活性。

Abstract: Reward design remains a central bottleneck for autonomous robot policy improvement, especially in long-horizon manipulation tasks where sparse success labels provide too little signal and binary preferences collapse many competing notions of quality into one ambiguous signal. We introduce Freeform Preference Learning (FPL), a method for learning robot policies from freeform human preferences. Rather than asking annotators which of two trajectories is better overall, FPL lets them define natural-language preference axes, such as speed, safety, quality of placement, or carefulness, and provide pairwise preferences along each axis. These annotations are used to learn a language-conditioned reward model that maps a trajectory and preference label to an axis-specific reward. We use this model to train a reward-conditioned policy that optimizes across the multiple human-specified dimensions. Across four real-world and two simulated long-horizon manipulation tasks, FPL improves over sparse-reward and binary-preference methods by 38 percentage points. Beyond improved performance, FPL learns dense progress signals without explicit subtask segmentation, shows compositionality of behavior not present in the data, and allows users to steer the policy towards different behaviors at test time without retraining. Blog post with videos available at https://freeform-pl.github.io/fpl.website/

</details>


### [261] [DVG-WM: Disentangled Video Generation Enables Efficient Embodied World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32028)
*Ziyu Shan,Zhenyu Wu,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了解耦视频生成世界模型(DVG-WM)，通过将世界建模分解为动态学习和视觉合成，解决了当前视频生成方法在机器人操作中推理速度慢和预测粗糙的问题，实现了3.97倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于视频的具身世界模型存在根本性纠缠：准确建模动态需要低级时间推理，而生成高分辨率帧需要高级语义的视觉合成，导致推理速度慢或预测过于粗糙无法保留接触细节。

Method: 提出DVG-WM框架，将世界建模明确分解为动态学习和视觉合成。模型基于初始观察和语言指令首先生成中间视觉状态序列预览物理交互，然后细化获得高保真视频。采用级联机制，使用流匹配将动态直接映射到视频潜在空间，并引入潜在退化机制重新生成接触细节。

Result: 在LIBERO和真实世界平台上的实验表明，该方法在改善视频质量的同时实现了高达3.97倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: 解耦视频生成可以作为机器人操作的高效具身世界模型，有效解决了动态建模与视觉合成之间的纠缠问题。

Abstract: Video-based embodied world models provide an appealing substrate for robotic manipulation by predicting future states, yet current approaches remain limited by a fundamental entanglement: accurately modeling dynamics typically requires low-level temporal reasoning, while producing high-resolution frames demands expansive visual synthesis according to high-level semantics. This entanglement results in slow inference speed for iterative planning or too coarse predictions to retain contact-rich details. To solve this dilemma, we present Disentangled Video Generation World Model (DVG-WM), an efficient framework that explicitly decomposes world modeling into dynamics learning and visual synthesis. Conditioned on an initial observation and a language instruction, our model first generates a plausible sequence of intermediate visual states to preview the physical interaction and refines them to obtain high-fidelity videos. Furthermore, an efficient cascading mechanism is proposed, where DVG-WM uses flow matching to directly map the dynamics to video latents, and introduces a latent degradation mechanism to regenerate contact-rich details. Experiments on LIBERO and real-world platforms demonstrate improved video quality with up to 3.97 times acceleration, validating that disentangled video generation can be an efficient embodied world model for robotic manipulation.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [262] [CALO: Constraint-Aware Learning Optimization for Joint Resource Allocation in Double-Active RIS-Assisted Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30803)
*Alaa S. Arabiyat,Mohammad J. Abdel-Rahman*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: CALO框架通过约束保持变换和直通估计器实现双主动RIS系统的实时资源分配，显著提升可行性和速率，同时大幅降低计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双主动RIS系统的联合资源分配因变量耦合导致传统方法计算复杂，难以实时应用。

Method: CALO将决策变量转为分组分数表示，通过约束保持变换映射物理资源，结合直通估计器处理离散变量，采用基于后悔的目标函数超越BCD性能。

Result: 仿真显示CALO在所有配置中100%可行，城乡场景速率优于BCD，在线推理时间减少数量级。

Conclusion: 结构感知学习有效实现主动多RIS系统的可行且实时优化。

Abstract: Double-active reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-assisted wireless systems can improve coverage and achievable rate in blockage-dominated environments. Still, their joint resource allocation is challenging due to the coupling among RIS placement, amplification power allocation, and reflecting-element assignment. The resulting problem is linearly constrained, non-convex, and involves both continuous and discrete variables, making conventional iterative solvers such as block coordinate descent (BCD) computationally expensive for real-time deployment. This paper proposes a \underline{c}onstraint-\underline{a}ware \underline{l}earning \underline{o}ptimization (CALO) framework for data-driven joint resource allocation in double-active RIS-assisted networks. CALO reformulates the decision variables into grouped fractional representations and maps them to physical resources through constraint-preserving transformations, ensuring that distance, power, and element-budget constraints are satisfied by construction. A straight-through estimator is incorporated to enable differentiable learning over discrete reflecting-element assignments, while a regret-driven hinge objective uses the BCD solution as a reference and encourages performance improvement beyond solver imitation. Simulation results show that CALO achieves $100\%$ feasibility across all tested configurations, improves the achievable rate over BCD in both urban and rural scenarios, and reduces online inference time by orders of magnitude. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of structure-aware learning for feasible and real-time optimization in active multi-RIS wireless systems.

</details>


### [263] [A Practical Implementation of Day-3 Cooperative Intersection with Automated Connected Mini-Cars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30838)
*Lorenzo Farina,Vittorio Todisco,Federico Gavioli,Salvatore Iandolo,Francesco Moretti,Giuseppe Perrone,Matteo Piccoli,Francesco Raviglione,Marco Rapelli,Antonio Solida,Claudio Casetti,Paolo Burgio,Carlo Augusto Grazia,Alessandro Bazzi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究通过联网自动驾驶迷你车实时演示合作路口管理，验证了无停车通过路口的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交通信号灯导致路口延误和停车，合作路口管理算法虽被广泛研究但实验验证有限，需实际演示验证其应用潜力。

Method: 构建1:10比例自动驾驶迷你车测试平台，集成无线通信模块与中央控制器，车辆通过消息交互动态调整轨迹，实现无停车通过路口。

Result: 成功实现多车实时协作通过路口的演示，验证了合作控制算法在动态环境中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该实验平台整合自动驾驶、通信与合作控制技术，为未来智能交通系统的合作路口管理提供了可验证的实践框架。

Abstract: Cooperative driving enabled by connected and automated vehicles is expected to improve traffic efficiency and safety, particularly at intersections where traditional control mechanisms such as traffic lights introduce delays and unnecessary stops. Although cooperative intersection management algorithms have been widely studied, experimental demonstrations remain limited. This paper presents a real-time demonstration of cooperative intersection management using connected autonomous mini-cars. The testbed consists of multiple 1:10 scale vehicles equipped with autonomous driving capabilities and wireless communication modules that interact with a centralized controller responsible for scheduling their crossing of the intersection. Vehicles approaching the intersection exchange messages with the controller to set the appropriate mobility profile to traverse the intersection without stopping. The demonstration integrates autonomous driving, wireless communication, and cooperative control in a single experimental platform, providing a practical environment for validating cooperative intersection management concepts for future intelligent transportation systems.

</details>


### [264] [Budget-Adaptive Routing: Skipping the Weak When the Strong Answers Anyway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30919)
*Wei Geng,Nitinder Mohan,Jörg Ott*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种预算自适应的边缘-云推理路由方法，通过根据卸载预算动态选择弱跳过或弱条件路由策略，在减少延迟的同时提升精度，甚至在某些情况下超越强模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘-云推理系统通常将路由估计器置于弱检测器之后，导致即使帧最终被卸载到云端，弱前向传递仍需运行。这种"弱条件"设计在卸载预算变化时可能不是最优的，存在计算资源浪费问题。

Method: 提出了两个核心创新：1）竞争性弱跳过估计器（0.153 GFLOPs，比弱检测器轻约29倍），直接从原始像素提取路由信号；2）预算自适应路由，通过两个离线调整的阈值根据卸载预算在弱跳过和弱条件放置之间动态选择。

Result: 在PASCAL VOC数据集上，预算自适应路由器在整个运行范围内追踪两种固定放置的上限精度包络；每帧延迟最多减少19.1毫秒（约30%降低）；在某些运行点上比强模型强1.7个百分点，但计算量更少。

Conclusion: 该方法优于现有SOTA方法，在保持高精度的同时显著降低延迟，并在某些情况下以更低计算成本超越强模型，为边缘-云推理协作提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Edge-cloud inference collaborations are often designed with a routing estimator that decides whether to offload each frame from weak models at the edge to stronger models in the cloud. Existing systems place the routing estimator after the weak detector, so the weak forward pass still runs even on frames that are later offloaded. In this paper, we argue that this weak-conditioned design can be suboptimal when the offload budget varies. First, we present a competitive weak-skipping estimator (0.153 GFLOPs, about 29x lighter than the weak detector at 4.49 GFLOPs) that extracts routing signal from raw pixels, outperforming the common after-weak placement weak-conditioned baselines. Second, we show that neither weak-skipping nor weak-conditioned placement dominates across the full operating curve, and we propose budget-adaptive routing, which selects between them by offload budget via two offline-tuned thresholds. On PASCAL VOC, our budget-adaptive router traces the upper accuracy envelope of both fixed placements across the operating range. Our method reduces per-frame latency by up to 19.1 ms (about 30% lower at rho = 0.9). Besides outperforming SOTA methods, it is surprisingly stronger than the strong model (+1.7 pp over the strong model's peak mAP) at some operating points with far less compute. Artifacts are available at https://github.com/ViGeng/bgt-ada

</details>


### [265] [Beyond Wireless Security: Covert Communications in Large Language Model-enabled Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31016)
*Yuanai Xie,Jiaxin Chen,Zhaozhi Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对6G时代大语言模型赋能边缘网络（LLMENs）面临的独特安全挑战，提出一种结合隐蔽通信与计算的解决方案，通过优化通信和计算流程，在保障隐私安全的同时显著降低延迟，并通过案例研究验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMENs因大语言模型的高计算复杂度、持续用户交互及电磁信息泄露风险，面临窃听、干扰、提示投毒/注入等新型安全威胁，而现有防御措施开销过大，亟需高效且全面的安全隐私保护方案。

Method: 提出隐蔽通信（CC）与计算协同框架，通过优化无线信道交互和计算过程实现隐蔽性；设计多种补充方案增强框架隐蔽性；构建以最小化总延迟为目标的案例研究模型，在严格通信与计算安全约束下验证方案性能。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提方法在满足严格安全需求的前提下，有效提升了LLM任务的隐私保护水平和执行效率，总延迟显著降低。

Conclusion: 该隐蔽通信与计算方案为LLMENs提供了兼顾安全性与效率的可行路径，解决了传统防御方法的高开销问题，为6G时代AI服务的规模化部署奠定安全基础。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-enabled edge networks (LLMENs) offer mobile users high-quality and low-latency AI-generated content services in the 6G era. However, unlike typical edge networks, LLMENs present unique security challenges due to the inherent complexity of LLMs, their high computational overhead, and continuous interactions with users. Specifically, both frequent user interactions (i.e., queries and responses) over wireless channels and potential electromagnetic information leakage from intensive LLM computations make LLMENs susceptible to various security threats, such as eavesdropping, jamming, prompt poisoning, and prompt injection attacks. Since existing countermeasures against these attacks often incur prohibitive overhead, developing holistic, efficient, and secure privacy protections for LLMENs is crucial. This article first reviews the vulnerabilities of LLMENs, outlines various attacks, and analyzes the drawbacks of existing countermeasures. To overcome these limitations, we propose a covert communications (CC) and computations approach to enhance both the overall security and efficiency of LLMENs. Furthermore, various supplementary solutions are developed to improve the covertness of this framework. Finally, our approach is further evaluated through a case study where the total latency is minimized under stringent communication and computational security requirements. Numerical results demonstrate the proposed approach's effectiveness in enhancing both privacy protection and the execution efficiency of LLM tasks.

</details>


### [266] [Support of Teleoperated Driving with 5G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31512)
*M. Carmen Lucas-Estañ,Baldomero Coll-Perales,Mohammad Irfan Khan,Sergei S. Avedisov,Onur Altintas,Javier Gozalvez,Miguel Sepulcre*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 5G网络支持远程驾驶（ToD）的可行性依赖于带宽及时分双工（TDD）帧结构，扩展车辆需降低视频比特率，传统集中式部署可能因互联网延迟难以满足严格延迟要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在评估5G网络能否满足远程驾驶对可靠低延迟通信的需求，以支持自动驾驶在复杂场景下的远程接管。

Method: 通过分析5G带宽分配和TDD帧结构对上下行传输的影响，并评估扩展车辆数量时的视频比特率调整策略，同时考察传统集中式网络部署的延迟问题。

Result: 5G支持ToD的可行性受带宽和TDD结构限制；扩展车辆需降低视频比特率；传统部署因互联网连接延迟可能无法满足严格延迟要求。

Conclusion: 5G网络在优化带宽和TDD配置下可行，但需降低视频比特率以扩展规模，并考虑去中心化部署以解决延迟瓶颈。

Abstract: Teleoperated driving (ToD) can support autonomous driving under complex or unexpected traffic scenarios that an autonomous vehicle may not understand or be able to handle. In ToD, autonomous vehicles transmit video feeds and perception data to the remote control center. The operator uses this data to understand the driving environment and remotely control the vehicle that can take over the control once the scenario is resolved. ToD requires reliable and low latency communications between the vehicle and the ToD control center. This study analyzes the feasibility to support ToD with 5G networks. The study demonstrates that the feasibility strongly depends on the bandwidth and the Time Division Duplexing (TDD) frame structure that conditions how the bandwidth is distributed between uplink and downlink transmissions. The study also shows that scaling the number of 5G-supported ToD vehicles requires the vehicles to reduce the video bitrates. The study also shows that traditional centralized 5G network deployments may be challenged by some of the most stringent ToD latency requirements due to the latency introduced by the Internet connection to the ToD control center.

</details>


### [267] [LEO Satellite Network Orchestration with Heterogeneous Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31950)
*Aruna Jayarajan,N. Cameron Matson,Karthikeyan Sundaresan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: NEO-GNN提出基于异构图神经网络的LEO卫星-地面网络实时编排框架，通过动态时空建模和无监督训练，实现全地面覆盖、负载均衡及高鲁棒性，优于传统优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星星座的动态性导致网络协调复杂，现有本地关联策略（如最近卫星或贪心负载选择）存在覆盖不全或负载失衡问题；集中式优化虽能平衡目标但计算开销大，难以实时部署。

Method: 将卫星-地面网络建模为动态时空图，采用异构图神经网络区分卫星、网关和地面单元节点类型，通过定制损失函数无监督训练以平衡覆盖与资源利用率，推理阶段生成实时关联决策。

Result: 实现完整地面单元覆盖，提升流量需求满足度，动态可见性和部分卫星故障下保持鲁棒性，计算效率显著优于传统优化方法。

Conclusion: NEO-GNN为弯管式LEO卫星系统提供可扩展、高效的实时网络编排解决方案，克服了传统方法在计算复杂度和动态适应性上的局限。

Abstract: Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite constellations are becoming essential for expanding global Internet access, especially in remote and under-served areas. However, their highly dynamic nature, arising from network mobility, introduces complex coordination challenges between the dynamic satellites and the ground nodes (gateways and terrestrial devices). This is underscored by limited satellite visibility windows and spatially imbalanced user traffic demands. Local association (cell-satellite-gateway) strategies, such as nearest-satellite or greedy load-based selection, result in partial terrestrial coverage or lead to load imbalance that affects traffic demand fulfillment. Network-driven orchestration through centralized optimization can strike an efficient balance between these two key objectives, but is often computationally intensive for periodic operation and real-time deployment. This work presents a learning-based network orchestration framework, NEO-GNN, that models a satellite-ground network as a dynamic spatiotemporal graph. In contrast to prior works, it employs a heterogeneous Graph Neural Network (GNN), where satellites, gateways, and ground cells are modeled as distinct node types to capture their varied visibility and networking capabilities. They are trained in an unsupervised manner using tailored loss functions to balance the dual requirements of coverage and utilization, and produce efficient, real-time association decisions during inference. Evaluations show that NEO-GNN delivers complete ground-cell coverage, improves traffic demand satisfaction through balanced satellite and gateway use, and remains robust under dynamic visibility and partial satellite failures. NEO-GNN provides a scalable and efficient alternative to traditional optimization methods for real-time network orchestration in bent-pipe LEO satellite systems.

</details>


### [268] [Semantic Leakage and Privacy Preservation in Relay-Assisted Semantic Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31973)
*Yalin E. Sagduyu,Tugba Erpek,Aylin Yener,Sennur Ulukus*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了中继辅助语义通信系统中的隐私漏洞，提出了一种迭代对抗训练框架，通过优化合法系统与中继窃听功能的博弈，在保持合法接收者性能的同时有效抑制中继的语义推断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中继节点在语义通信中直接处理学习到的潜在表示，即使无原始数据访问权限，仍能可靠推断语义并重建信号，暴露了语义表示的隐私风险。

Method: 设计迭代对抗训练框架，交替优化中继窃听函数与合法系统，生成既保留合法接收者解码性能又降低中继语义推断能力的语义表示。

Result: 在不同信道条件下显著拉大合法接收者与窃听者间的语义准确率差距，同时保持高重建保真度实现隐蔽式保护。

Conclusion: 所提方法为语义通信系统提供了一种兼顾性能与隐私的解决方案，证明通过对抗训练可有效缓解语义表示的隐私泄露问题。

Abstract: Semantic communication (SemCom) has emerged as a promising paradigm in which the transmission of task-relevant information is prioritized over raw data, enabling efficient and robust communication under resource and channel constraints. In this paper, the privacy implications of relay-assisted SemCom systems are studied, where the intermediate relay node operates directly on learned latent representations. It is shown that the relay, even without access to source data, can reliably infer semantic meaning and reconstruct signals with performance comparable to that of the legitimate receiver, revealing a fundamental privacy vulnerability of semantic representations. To address this issue, an iterative adversarial training framework is proposed in which a strong, adaptively trained eavesdropper at the relay is explicitly accounted for. The proposed approach alternates between optimizing the relay's eavesdropping function and the legitimate system, resulting in representations that preserve semantic decoding performance at the intended receiver while degrading semantic inference at the relay. The semantic accuracy gap between the legitimate receiver and the eavesdropper is significantly enlarged across channel conditions. Importantly, this protection is achieved in a stealthy manner, with high reconstruction fidelity maintained while semantic leakage is selectively suppressed.

</details>


### [269] [Semantic-Aware Multiple Access via Spatial Redundancy Exploitation for Uplink-Dominant 6G Use Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31715)
*Hamidreza Mazandarani,Masoud Shokrnezhad,Tarik Taleb,Onur Günlü*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种语义感知多接入方案，通过利用车辆间空间冗余减少6G上行链路中的重复传输，采用两阶段方法：V2V共享观察补丁计算冗余，再传输非冗余语义重要补丁至基站。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义通信方法独立处理用户，未利用邻近设备观察数据的空间冗余，导致上行传输效率低下，难以满足6G高流量视觉数据传输需求。

Method: 设计两阶段方案：1) 车辆通过V2V链路共享选定观察补丁以计算空间冗余；2) 仅向基站传输语义重要且非冗余的图像补丁，结合邻车互补视图完成重建。

Result: 在密集城市车辆场景中，该方法显著提高了实现高保真重建的用户比例，验证了语义感知多接入对提升传输效率的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方案为6G上行系统提供了可持续的资源高效解决方案，通过空间冗余利用和语义感知优化，有望支撑未来高流量视觉数据应用。

Abstract: Emerging uplink-dominant 6G use cases, such as cooperative vehicular streaming, require efficient transmission of high-volume visual data over limited wireless resources. While semantic communications can reduce traffic by prioritizing task-relevant content, most existing approaches treat users independently and therefore overlook spatial redundancy among nearby devices' observations. This paper proposes a semantic-aware multiple access scheme that exploits overlapping fields of view among vehicular users to reduce redundant uplink transmissions. We formulate a joint perception and transmission control problem in which users decide which image patches to transmit, when to transmit them, and over which channel, subject to communication constraints. To address the resulting complexity, we introduce a practical two-phase approach. First, nearby vehicles share selected observation patches over Vehicle-to-Vehicle (V2V) links to calculate inter-user spatial redundancy. Second, users transmit only semantically important, non-redundant patches to the base station, where observations can be reconstructed using the received patches and complementary views from neighboring vehicles. Simulation results in a dense urban vehicular scenario demonstrate that our approach improves the proportion of users who achieve high-fidelity reconstruction, highlighting the potential of semantic-aware multiple access for sustainable and resource-efficient 6G uplink systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [270] [What Drives Interactive Improvement from Feedback?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30774)
*Bartłomiej Cupiał,Jan Łojek,Mikołaj Garstecki,Szymon Pobłocki,Alicja Ziarko,Piotr Miłoś*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了自然语言反馈在多轮语言代理中的实际效果，发现多轮改进往往并非来自反馈使用，而是重复尝试的结果。自生成反馈效果有限，而高质量外部教师反馈能产生显著增益。关键在于学生模型使用反馈的能力，而非反馈本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮语言代理设置中，最终准确率的提升可能源于有用反馈，也可能来自重新采样、格式修正或额外的测试时计算。需要区分这些效应，以评估反馈的真实价值。

Method: 引入受控的学生-教师协议，在Omni-MATH、Codeforces、BBEH Linguini和ARC-AGI1数据集上评估13个开放权重模型，比较外部反馈、自反馈和无引导自我改进，并变化交互历史、任务难度和教师对特权任务信息的访问权限。

Result: 多轮改进往往不是反馈使用的证据：自生成反馈添加的很少，而最强外部教师产生更大的反馈特定增益。交互增益更多由学生使用反馈的能力驱动，而非教师身份，尽管教师选择对固定学生仍然重要。

Conclusion: 基于反馈的代理应与重复尝试基线进行评估，使用反馈的能力而非反馈可用性是交互改进的中央瓶颈。研究发布了受控学生-教师评估框架。

Abstract: We study when natural-language feedback produces improvement beyond the gains obtainable from repeated attempts alone. In multi-turn language agent setting, higher final accuracy can reflect useful feedback, but it can also arise from resampling, format correction, or additional test-time computation. To separate these effects, we introduce a controlled student-teacher protocol across Omni-MATH, Codeforces, BBEH Linguini, and ARC-AGI1, evaluating thirteen open-weight models in both student and teacher roles. We compare external feedback, self-feedback, and unguided self-refinement, while varying interaction history, task difficulty, and teacher access to privileged task information. Across settings, we find that multi-turn improvement is often not evidence of feedback use: self-generated feedback adds little beyond unguided self-refinement, whereas the strongest external teachers produce substantially larger feedback-specific gains, suggesting that useful feedback must provide guidance beyond generic retry. Dense student-teacher interaction matrices further show that interactive gains are driven more by the student's ability to use feedback than by the teacher's identity, although teacher choice remains important for a fixed student. These results suggest that feedback-based agents should be evaluated against repeated-attempt baselines, and that ability to act on feedback, not merely feedback availability, is a central bottleneck for interactive improvement. We release our controlled student-teacher evaluation framework at https://j-lojek.github.io/feedback-generation-is-a-bottleneck/.

</details>


### [271] [Contrastive Reflection for Iterative Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30840)
*Derek Koh,Jinghui Mo,Benjamin H. Le,Jiening Zhan,Baofen Zheng,Kevin Bevis,Nathaniel C. Owen,Lauren Elizabeth Charney,Wenqiong Liu,Jingwei Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出对比反射框架，通过结构化轨迹分析和教师LLM引导的迭代优化，提升信息检索代理的提示词性能，在HotpotQA上实现准确率从51.4%到60.4%的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示优化方法缺乏对失败行为的精准诊断和验证机制，工程师难以定位问题、保留有效行为并避免回归，导致优化过程效率低下且不可控。

Method: 构建对比反射循环：1) 提取任务轨迹（检索/推理路径、评分维度）；2) 识别错误行为切片并匹配邻近成功案例；3) 教师LLM生成针对性修改建议；4) 通过验证集性能提升和回归检查筛选有效修改。

Result: 在HotpotQA数据集上，单轮对比修复使精确匹配准确率提升9个百分点（51.4%→60.4%），优于仅用失败案例（+3.2%）和随机证据（+1.7%）的变体，且与MIPROv2（59.4%）、GEPA（57.0%）等现代方法性能接近。

Conclusion: 该框架提供可解释的优化闭环，将提示修复转化为可验证的工程调试过程，显著提升IR代理系统的鲁棒性和开发效率。

Abstract: LLM agents are becoming central to information retrieval: they issue retrieval queries, synthesize answers, and increasingly serve as judges for IR evaluation. Improving the prompts that control these agents is an optimization problem, but in applied IR settings it often looks less like blind search and more like debugging. Engineers need to know which behavior failed, which nearby behavior still worked, what distinguishes the two, and whether a prompt edit improves held-out quality without introducing regressions.
  We present Contrastive Reflection, an iterative prompt-optimization framework for agentic IR workflows. The framework starts from a task-centric quality definition: QA agents expose retrieval or reasoning traces, and grading agents expose dimension-level scores and rationales. These structured traces are used to identify error-anchored behavioral slices, add nearby successful examples from the same region, and ask a Teacher LLM to propose a targeted prompt edit. Candidate edits are accepted only when validation performance improves, optionally subject to regression checks. We instantiate the framework with a tree-based slice selector, but the contribution is the contrastive reflection loop rather than the tree itself.
  On a public HotpotQA retrieval-augmented QA setup, one tree-selected contrastive repair improves held-out exact-match accuracy from 51.4% to 60.4%. Failure-only and random-evidence variants improve less and break more previously correct examples. A light instruction-only comparison places the method near modern prompt optimizers: MIPROv2 reaches 59.4% and GEPA 57.0%. The result is an interpretable optimization loop for IR agents, aimed at making prompt repair more inspectable and validation-driven.

</details>


### [272] [How Can AI Find My Model? A Model-Finding Experimental Study Considering Data Formats, Embeddings, and Retrieval Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30846)
*Jhon G. Botello,Jose J. Padilla,Erika Frydenlund,Krzysztof Rechowicz,Eric Weisel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验评估了数据表示、Transformer嵌入模型及检索策略对自然语言查询下仿真模型发现的影响，发现数据表示和重排序方法对复杂查询效果显著，为AI驱动模型发现提供了基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在建模与仿真领域，从大量共存模型中识别符合特定建模意图的可重用模型存在挑战。传统方法难以处理语义层面的匹配需求，而基于检索的AI技术为这一问题提供了潜在解决方案。

Method: 通过实验系统评估了三种关键因素：(1) 不同数据表示方法的效果；(2) 开源Transformer嵌入模型的性能；(3) 多种检索策略（含重排序）对查询结果的影响。采用Recall@5和nDCG@5等标准信息检索指标，在多种查询类型上进行验证。

Result: 实验表明：① 数据表示方式对检索效果有显著影响；② 开源嵌入模型可达到高性能；③ 重排序方法在复杂查询场景中作用尤为突出，能显著提升结果相关性。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了AI驱动模型发现的基准框架，验证了语义检索技术的有效性，为未来实现AI驱动的可组合性与互操作性奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: Discovering simulation models for reuse remains a fundamental challenge in Modeling and Simulation (M&S). When many models coexist, identifying those that align with a given modeling intent remains difficult. Recent advances in Artificial Intelligence (AI), particularly retrieval-based approaches, offer a promising pathway to operate at this semantic layer. In this paper, we present an experimental study investigating the impact of data representation, transformer-based embedding models, and retrieval strategies on the discovery of simulation models using natural language queries. We evaluated performance across multiple query types using standard information retrieval metrics, including recall@5 and nDCG@5. Results show that data representation matters, open-source embedding models can achieve high performance, and reranking methods are important, especially as query complexity increases. This work provides a baseline for AI-driven model discovery and discusses its role in advancing toward AI-driven composability and interoperability.

</details>


### [273] [BayesBench: Evaluating LLM Belief Trajectories Under Multi-Turn Evidence Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30850)
*Ankur Samanta,Akshayaa Magesh,Tal Lancewicki,Ayush Jain,Youliang Yu,Paul Sajda,Kaveh Hassani,Aditya Modi,Daniel R. Jiang,Yonathan Efroni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大模型多轮对话信念更新评估缺失的问题，构建BayesBench基准，在3B-70B共7个大模型上测试3类递进式贝叶斯任务，发现规模提升能增强潜变量推断和证据积累能力，部分更新符合贝叶斯后验，但该能力无法稳定迁移到下游预测，存在潜变量推断与目标结果信念更新的能力鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型多轮对话中每轮新信息本应降低环境认知不确定性，理性决策需要推断未观测量并随证据积累更新信念，但现有评估仅针对单轮最终答案打分，完全忽略了多轮信念更新过程的考察，因此需要探究大模型的多轮信念更新是否符合理性贝叶斯推理者的表现，填补该评估空白。

Method: 构建BayesBench评估基准，包含三类复杂度递进的任务：1）贝叶斯估计：从序列证据中推断未知参数；2）贝叶斯预测：将潜变量的推断信念转化为结果预测；3）潜变量框架下的贝叶斯预测：观测值经过用户人设框架过滤，需联合推断潜状态和用户人设。在7个3B到70B规模的大语言模型上开展测试，对比模型信念更新与理性贝叶斯推理者的匹配程度。

Result: 模型规模扩大可提升潜变量推断和证据积累能力，部分情况下模型的信念更新能够匹配贝叶斯后验，但这种提升无法稳定迁移到下游预测任务中，暴露了潜变量结构推断和基于此理性更新目标结果信念之间的能力差距。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型的多轮贝叶斯信念更新能力存在断层：规模增长可提升潜变量推断和证据整合能力，但无法将推断能力稳定转化为下游预测的理性信念更新，未来需要针对该能力鸿沟设计优化方案，同时完善多轮推理过程的评估体系。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically deployed in multi-turn conversations, where each turn provides new evidence that should reduce epistemic uncertainty about their environment. Acting rationally then requires inferring the unobserved quantities that govern it and updating beliefs about them as evidence accumulates. Yet most evaluations only score the model's final-turn answer in a single-turn format, leaving this process unexamined. We ask how closely LLMs' belief updates match those of a rational Bayesian reasoner in multi-turn settings, and introduce BayesBench, a suite of simulation environments that probe this across three progressively complex tasks: (i) Bayesian estimation, where the model infers an unknown parameter from sequential evidence; (ii) Bayesian prediction, where the model turns inferred beliefs about a latent variable into outcome forecasts; and (iii) latent-framed Bayesian prediction, where observations are filtered through a user-persona framing, requiring joint inference over the latent state and the persona. Across seven LLMs (3B--70B), scaling improves latent inference and evidence accumulation, with updates occasionally matching the Bayesian posterior. However, these gains do not reliably carry over to downstream prediction, exposing a gap between inferring latent structure and using it to rationally update beliefs about the target outcome.

</details>


### [274] [When Does Learning to Stop Help? A Cost-Aware Study of Early Exits in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30852)
*Zhe Dong,Fang Qin,Manish Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 学习停止规则在推理模型中的应用效果因任务而异：在自由形式数学任务上优于简单阈值方法，但在选择题和困难任务上效果相当或更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在不同实例上花费的计算量不同，但何时学习到的停止规则优于简单的置信度或收敛阈值仍不清楚。

Method: LearnStop是一种无隐藏状态的检查点停止器，在固定预算检查点处从当前推理前缀探测简短答案，并根据在线特征（如答案置信度、熵、前缀投票份额、答案稳定性和回溯标记密度）预测前缀正确性。

Result: 在18个任务-模型设置上测试（包括GSM8K、MATH-500、MMLU-Pro、AIME-90、GPQA等），在自由形式数学上学习停止优于固定预算前沿，在选择题和非常困难设置上标量置信度、熵或稳定性规则具有竞争力或更强。

Conclusion: 学习停止不是标量退出的通用替代品，其价值取决于轨迹结构。当许多问题在完整预算前变得正确但没有单一的可靠标量停止信号时，学习停止是有用的；当置信度或答案收敛已经解决了停止问题时，其好处基本消失。

Abstract: Reasoning models spend different amounts of useful computation across instances, but it remains unclear when a learned stopping rule improves over simple confidence or convergence thresholds. We study this question with LearnStop, a hidden-state-free checkpoint stopper for reasoning language models. At fixed budget checkpoints, LearnStop probes a short answer from the current reasoning prefix and predicts prefix correctness from online features such as answer confidence, entropy, prefix vote share, answer stability, and backtracking-marker density. Across 18 task-model settings spanning GSM8K, MATH-500, MMLU-Pro, AIME-90, GPQA, Qwen3, and DeepSeek-R1 distillations, the answer is task-dependent. On free-form math, learned multi-feature stopping improves the fixed-budget frontier and often beats scalar exits: on GSM8K with Qwen3-32B, the empirical frontier reaches a post-hoc peak adapt gain of +0.157, validation-selected operating points preserve positive gains, and the paired gain over the strongest scalar baseline is +0.028. On multiple-choice and very hard settings, scalar confidence, entropy, or stability rules are competitive or stronger. We therefore frame learned stopping not as a universal replacement for scalar exits, but as a tool whose value depends on trajectory structure. We further provide validation-selected operating points, paired bootstrap tests, finite-grid lost-correct risk calibration, cost accounting under KV-fork, prefix-cache, and black-box regimes, H100 serving profiles, checkpoint-schedule sweeps, transfer analyses, and robustness checks. The main practical finding is that learned stopping is useful when many questions become correct before full budget but do not exhibit a single reliable scalar stopping signal; its benefits largely disappear when confidence or answer convergence already solves the stopping problem.

</details>


### [275] [Beyond expert users: agents should help users construct preferences, not just elicit them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30863)
*Irena Saracay,Ludwig Schmidt,Carlos Guestrin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文指出AI代理系统假设用户是专家且偏好明确是不现实的。提出了CoPref模型和CoShop基准测试，评估发现现有模型准确率仅56%，主要问题在于交互未能帮助用户明确自己的偏好，而非找不到合适物品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理系统通常假设用户是专家，具有明确的偏好，当任务不明确时默认通过澄清问题来获取信息。然而这种假设不现实，因为用户往往缺乏足够的领域知识来形成完全明确的偏好，需要代理帮助其学习必要的领域知识才能形成偏好。

Method: 基于信息经济学中的搜索-经验-可信度框架，提出了CoPref模型来形式化描述用户如何基于代理对话行为构建偏好。随后在智能推荐系统领域具体研究这些想法，提出了CoShop交互式基准测试，其中代理与CoPref用户对话并做出推荐。

Result: 评估了五个前沿模型，发现尽管有五轮交互，没有代理的准确率超过56%。失败的原因并非代理寻找物品的能力，而是交互过程很少扩展用户对自己想要什么的认知。

Conclusion: AI代理系统的性能取决于其能否帮助用户获得明确任务所需的知识，而不仅仅是询问澄清问题。现有系统需要改进交互方式，以更好地帮助用户构建和明确自己的偏好。

Abstract: Agents typically assume an expert user -- one with well-formed preferences about what they want -- and default to clarifying questions whenever the task is underspecified. We argue this assumption is unrealistic. Users often lack the domain knowledge to have completely specified preferences; if asked about their preference on some feature, the user may be unable to answer without the agent helping the user to learn some domain knowledge needed to form a preference for that feature, e.g., via examples or explanations. To formalize these principles, we draw on the Search-Experience-Credence framework from Information Economics to introduce CoPref, a model of how users construct preferences based on agent dialog actions. We then study these ideas concretely in agentic recommender systems, proposing CoShop, an interactive benchmark. In CoShop, an agent converses with and makes recommendations for a CoPref user. The agent's performance depends on whether it can help the user gain the knowledge needed to specify the task well. Evaluating five frontier models, we find that no agent exceeds 56% accuracy on CoShop despite five turns of interaction. Failures stem not from agents' ability to find items, but from how little the interaction expands what users know about what they want.

</details>


### [276] [Investigating Multi-Agent Deliberation in Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30906)
*Cor Steging,Ludi van Leeuwen,Tadeusz Zbiegień*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探索基于大语言模型的多智能体协商框架在法律推理任务中的应用，提出两种受法庭程序和法理论证启发的新方法。实验表明多智能体系统虽整体性能与基线模型相当，但能生成显著不同的答案，并在需多视角批判性思考的场景中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在法律领域的应用潜力巨大，但现有研究多集中于单一大语言模型，而基于多智能体协商（MAD）的法律推理方法尚未充分探索。传统单一模型难以处理需多角度分析的法律问题，亟需创新框架提升AI在法律场景中的适用性。

Method: 提出两种新型多智能体框架：1) 模拟法庭程序的对抗式协商机制；2) 基于法律论证结构的协作式推理流程。通过设计智能体角色分工与交互规则，实现法律问题的多维度分析。

Result: 在法律及非法理基准测试中：1) 多智能体系统整体准确率与基线LLM持平；2) 答案多样性显著提升，能解决基线模型失效的案例；3) 定性评估显示其在需批判性思维、多视角分析的任务中优势明显。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统为法律AI开辟新方向，法理启发的协商机制可增强AI处理复杂法律问题的能力。未来需进一步优化智能体协作效率，推动AI在法律实务中的深度应用。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence is increasingly applied to the field of law, and has the potential to increase access to justice. One particular movement that is gaining traction is that of agentic AI, wherein AI agents, based on Large Language Models (LLMs) can take autonomous actions. In particular, multi-agent approaches in the legal domain remain largely unexplored. In this paper, we investigate multi-agent deliberation methods for legal reasoning tasks using LLMs. We explore multi-agent deliberation (MAD) and introduce two novel multi-agent frameworks inspired by courtroom procedures and legal argumentation. Our experiments on both legal and non-legal benchmarks reveal that multi-agent frameworks achieve comparable overall performance to baseline large language models, but produce significantly distinct answers. Notably, these approaches can successfully solve cases that the baseline fails to address, and vice versa. We conduct a qualitative evaluation and highlight scenarios where multi-agent frameworks outperform monolithic approaches. For example, multi-agent approaches appear better suited for answering questions that require critical thinking from multiple perspectives. Our work positions multi-agent systems as a promising direction for AI in the legal domain, while demonstrating the potential of law-inspired multi-agent approaches for deliberation.

</details>


### [277] [Why Solve It Twice? Hierarchical Accumulation of Skills for Transfer-Efficient ML Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30911)
*Yongbin Kim,Yashar Talebirad,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HASTE通过分层知识管理系统（全局/领域/竞赛特定）和LLM驱动抽象，解决ML工程代理重复发现已知技术的问题，显著提升奖牌率并减少计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ML工程代理在每次竞赛中从零开始，导致重复发现已知技术、浪费计算资源。需要一种跨竞赛知识复用机制提升效率。

Method: 提出HASTE分层多智能体系统：1) 三层知识范围（全局/领域/竞赛特定）对应不同智能体层级；2) 协调器管理领域专家并通过LLM实现跨层抽象学习；3) 实验采用消融研究对比分层加载与扁平加载，并在MLE-Bench Lite基准测试中验证。

Result: 1. 分层加载在8项竞赛中实现100%奖牌率（扁平加载仅62.5%），输出token减少50%；2. MLE-Bench Lite测试中达77.3%奖牌率（Claude Sonnet 4.6，12h/竞赛）；3. 热启动比冷启动减少52%迭代次数，技能保留率从42%提升至85%（50+技能时）。

Conclusion: 结构化知识组织可部分替代模型能力和计算预算，通过跨竞赛知识复用显著提升ML工程代理的效率与性能。

Abstract: ML engineering agents waste compute rediscovering known techniques because every competition is a cold start. We present HASTE, a hierarchical multi-agent system that organizes cross-competition knowledge into three scope tiers (global, domain, and competition-specific), each coupled to a matching agent level. An orchestrator coordinates domain specialists and promotes learning between tiers via LLM-driven abstraction. A controlled ablation provides evidence for scoped loading: holding a 159-skill inventory constant across 8 competitions, tiered loading achieves a 100% medal rate while flat loading reaches only 62.5%, the same medal rate as loading no skills, and consumes 2x the output tokens. On the full MLE-Bench Lite benchmark (22 Kaggle competitions), HASTE reaches a medal rate of 77.3% using Claude Sonnet 4.6 at 12h per competition. In a cold-start run, the system begins with no accumulated skills. In warm-start runs, it reloads skills learned from earlier competitions, using only global and domain-level skills for transfer across competitions. Warm starts use 52% fewer refinement iterations, and the fraction of proposed changes kept by the agent rises from 42% at low inventory to 85% once 50+ skills are available. These results suggest that better knowledge organization can partly substitute for model strength and compute budget in ML-engineering agents.

</details>


### [278] [RoPoLL: Robust Panel of LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30931)
*Anish Acharya,Kris W Pan,Brian Verkhovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RoPoLL通过鲁棒聚合提升LLM陪审团评估的抗污染能力，在多种污染场景下显著优于传统方法，同时实现参数效率突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估方法（PoLL）在存在任何污染时会产生无界偏差，即使增加评估者数量也无法解决，亟需鲁棒性更强的评估框架。

Method: 基于Huber污染模型形式化分析PoLL缺陷，提出RoPoLL方法：保留面板结构但用几何中位数替换传统聚合函数，实现无需调参且具备最优崩溃点(1/2)的鲁棒均值估计。

Result: 在13个开源模型、3个奖励模型基准和4种污染场景（最高50%污染率）中验证：RoPoLL在跨维度攻击中性能提升19%，对拜占庭攻击效果提升数量级；3个38B参数RoPoLL委员会在HelpSteer-2上超越675B的Mistral-Large-3达1.31倍，参数效率提升18倍。

Conclusion: RoPoLL有效解决LLM评估的污染敏感性问题，理论证明其达到参数率σ√(d/N)，实验验证其在计算效率与抗污染能力间取得最优平衡，为大规模语言模型评估提供新范式。

Abstract: The LLM Jury, a Panel of LLM Evaluators (PoLL) reporting consensus scores, has become a practical alternative to single-judge LLM evaluation, yet its statistical behavior remains poorly understood. We formalize the LLM Jury under the Huber contamination model and show that PoLL incurs unbounded bias
  under any positive contamination, regardless of jury size, whenever a single judge fails in a biased, LLM-typical way (mode collapse, sycophancy, safety refusal). Framing jury consensus as classical robust mean estimation, we propose RoPoLL (Robust Panel of LLM-as-Judge), which preserves the PoLL
  panel but replaces the aggregation function with a robust mean estimator, instantiated with the geometric median (GM): tuning-free, with the optimal finite-sample breakdown point 1/2. A finite-sample error bound and a matching information-theoretic minimax lower bound agree on the parametric rate
  sigma*sqrt(d/N) and differ on the breakdown floor by a factor of sqrt(d), a statistical-computational gap that polynomial-time RoPoLL pays relative to the intractable Tukey halfspace median. Across 13 open-weight judges (4B-675B), three reward-model benchmarks, and four corruption regimes at rates up
  to 50%, RoPoLL dominates PoLL on every biased corruption type: by about 19% on cross-dimensional attacks at matched compute, and by orders of magnitude on heavy-tailed Byzantine adversaries. A 3-judge RoPoLL committee at 38B beats Mistral-Large-3 (675B) by 1.31x on HelpSteer-2 under 30% bimodal-random
  corruption, an 18x parameter advantage at better accuracy; a Noisy-GT control confirms the premium is paid against biased contamination, not benign imprecision.

</details>


### [279] [AgRefactor: Self-Evolving Agentic Workflow for HLS Compatibility and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30949)
*Yang Zou,Zijian Ding,Yizhou Sun,Jason Cong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgRefactor是一个基于LLM的多智能体重构框架，通过自演进记忆系统和自动化工具集成，高效将复杂软件转换为HLS兼容代码，在多个真实基准上优于现有方法并实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高电平综合（HLS）虽能加速硬件设计，但将现实软件转换为可综合HLS代码面临语言限制和软硬件编程实践差异的挑战。现有自动和LLM重构方法存在灵活性不足、扩展性差、计算成本高等问题。

Method: 提出AgRefactor：基于LLM的多智能体重构工作流，包含自演进记忆系统（积累跨任务事实与策略知识），并集成自动重构工具以平衡LLM重写与高效工具转换，降低计算成本。

Result: 在11个真实世界基准（比先前最复杂案例长5-10倍）中的9个上，AgRefactor性能优于或匹配最先进自动重构工具和强LLM基线；进一步优化实现比最先进pragma调优工具6.51倍几何平均加速，比优化开源设计1.20倍加速，额外资源开销不足20%。

Conclusion: AgRefactor实现了完全自动化的软件到HLS代码重构，开源可用，通过多智能体协作和记忆机制显著提升了重构效率、可扩展性和性能，为HLS应用提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: High-Level Synthesis (HLS) provides a fast path from concepts to silicon, but converting real-world software into synthesizable HLS code remains challenging due to restrictive language support and the gap between software and hardware programming practices. Existing automated and LLM-based refactoring approaches partially address this problem, yet they often lack flexibility, struggle to scale, and incur high computational costs. We introduce AgRefactor, an LLM-based multi-agent workflow for refactoring software into HLS-compatible programs. AgRefactor incorporates a self-evolving memory system that accumulates and retrieves factual and strategic knowledge across tasks, improving robustness and efficiency on unseen programs. To reduce cost and enhance scalability, it integrates automated refactoring tools, enabling agents to balance LLM-driven rewrites with efficient tool-based transformations. On 9 out of 11 challenging real-world benchmarks, which are 5-10x longer than the most complex cases studied in prior work, AgRefactor outperforms or matches the state-of-the-art automated refactoring tool and a strong LLM-based baseline built on the same framework backbone. Further agentic performance optimization yields a 6.51x geometric mean speedup over the SoTA pragma tuning tool and a 1.20x speedup over optimized open-source designs with less than 20% extra resources. AgRefactor is fully-automated and open-sourced.

</details>


### [280] [Neuro-Bayesian-Symbolic Residual Attention Shallow Network: Explainable Deep Learning for Cybersecurity Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30953)
*Nicolaie Popescu-Bodorin,Madeleine Togher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出神经-贝叶斯-符号残差注意力浅层网络（NBS-RASN），一种混合浅层架构，用于开源生态系统的可解释网络安全风险评估。通过编码领域知识、因果推理和专家判断，在保持高置信度（0.79-0.97）的同时确保可解释性，挑战了深度学习必须依赖深度网络的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决深度模型在网络安全风险评估中因追求准确性而牺牲可解释性的核心问题。在高风险开源生态系统中，决策透明性至关重要，但现有深度模型常成为“黑箱”，缺乏可追溯性，难以满足监管和专家审查需求。

Method: 设计浅层混合网络：使用80个可解释神经元分布于12层，包含门控机制强制执行五个认识论公理（精度、因果性、可证伪性、透明性、完整性）作为硬约束；通过残差注意力和反馈循环实现深度学习特性，避免黑箱化；输出完全可分解分数（确定性加权组件 + 专家调整），每个调整可追溯至具体放大器（如爆炸半径、传播速度）。

Result: 在20个开源项目上验证，覆盖所有OWASP Top 10:2025类别和语言风险类别，置信度得分达0.79-0.97；实验证明可解释性由架构设计保证而非训练算法，浅层网络通过深度推理机制实现高性能。

Conclusion: 挑战了“深度学习需要深度网络”的传统假设，证明浅层网络结合结构化推理可在高风险网络安全场景中优于不透明模型，强调可解释性应作为核心设计原则而非事后补救措施。

Abstract: We introduce the Neuro-Bayesian-Symbolic Residual Attention Shallow Network (NBS-RASN), a hybrid neural architecture for explainable cybersecurity risk assessment in open-source ecosystems. Unlike deep models that trade interpretability for accuracy, our shallow network encodes domain knowledge, causal reasoning, and expert judgment as differentiable components. It uses 80 interpretable neurons across 12 layers, including a gatekeeper that enforces five epistemological axioms - precision, causality, falsifiability, transparency, and completeness - as hard constraints before propagation. Despite limited depth, the network exhibits deep-learning traits via residual attention and feedback loops, learning complex risk patterns without becoming a black box. It produces fully decomposable scores: a deterministic weighted component plus an expert adjustment, with each adjustment traceable to named amplifiers (blast radius, propagation speed, structural nature, default exposure, exploitation pattern, institutional criticality). We validate on 20 open-source projects covering all OWASP Top 10:2025 categories and language risk classes, achieving confidence scores of 0.79-0.97, and show that explainability is guaranteed by design, not by a training algorithm. This challenges the assumption that deep learning requires deep networks, proving that shallow networks with deep reasoning can outperform opaque models in high-stakes cybersecurity, where interpretability is essential.

</details>


### [281] [HyPOLE: Hyperproperty-Guided Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning under Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30966)
*Arshia Rafieioskouei,Tzu-Han Hsu,Matthew Lucas,Borzoo Bonakdarpour*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyPOLE是一个新颖的多智能体强化学习框架，通过结合形式化规范（HyperLTL）与集中训练分散执行（CTDE）技术，在部分可观测环境下实现去中心化策略合成，并在多个基准测试中展现出优于基线方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化规范在数学严谨性、目标/约束表达能力及策略定义方面具有显著优势，但这些优势在多智能体强化学习（MARL）中尚未被充分探索，尤其是在部分可观测环境下。

Method: 提出HyPOLE框架，将超属性（特别是HyperLTL时序逻辑）与CTDE技术相结合，用于合成去中心化策略。

Result: 在SMAC、MessySMAC和WildFire基准测试上的评估表明，HyPOLE相比基线方法具有明显优势。

Conclusion: HyPOLE成功利用形式化规范的优势，提升了部分可观测MARL环境的性能，为相关领域提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Formal specification is a powerful tool to guide the learning process and provides significant advantages over reward shaping: (1) mathematical rigor; (2) expressiveness to specify objectives and constraints, and (3) the ability to define tactics to achieve objectives. However, these benefits remain largely unexplored in the context of Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL). This paper introduces HyPOLE, a novel framework for MARL under partial observability, where learning is guided by the expressive power of the so-called hyperproperties and, in particular, the temporal logic HyperLTL. We integrate Centralized Training for Decentralized Execution (CTDE) techniques with HyPOLE to synthesize decentralized policies, and our evaluation on SMAC, MessySMAC, and WildFire benchmark demonstrates clear advantages over baselines.

</details>


### [282] [AgentBound: Verifiable Behavioral Governance for Autonomous AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30970)
*Anuj Kaul,Qianlong Lan,Pranay Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentBound是一个运行时治理框架，通过三个独立权威（委托授权、所有者签名的行为宪法、站点操作合同）对自主AI代理的每个操作进行评估，生成密码学可验证的治理收据，实现可独立验证的治理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理基础设施依赖身份联合和委托授权来认证工作负载和控制资源访问，但无法判断授权操作是否应在当前行为和操作上下文中执行，缺乏运行时行为监管能力。

Method: 提出AgentBound框架，使用三个独立权威评估每个提议的操作：委托授权、所有者签名的行为宪法和站点操作合同。通过形式化决策模型保守组合这些判断，决定操作是否应被允许、审查或拒绝。引入持续委托机制支持长期运行的代理，并生成密码学可验证的治理收据。

Result: 建立了形式化基础、系统架构和治理收据协议，开发了AgentBound-Bench基准框架用于评估治理正确性、权威组合和问责制。框架实现了可独立重放验证和政策溯源能力。

Conclusion: AgentBound不替代模型对齐，而是作为授权和执行之间的确定性治理层进行补充，将治理从需要信任的过程转变为可独立验证的过程，为自主AI代理提供了可验证的行为监管能力。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents increasingly perform consequential actions on behalf of human principals, including financial transactions, external communications, and enterprise workflows. Existing agent infrastructure relies on identity federation and delegated authorization to authenticate workloads and control resource access, but it cannot determine whether an authorized action should be executed under the current behavioral and operational context.
  We present AgentBound, a runtime governance framework that provides verifiable behavioral oversight for autonomous AI agents. AgentBound evaluates each proposed action using three independent authorities: delegated authorization, owner-signed behavioral constitutions, and site action contracts. Their judgments are conservatively composed through a formal decision model to determine whether an action should be permitted, reviewed, or denied before execution.
  To provide accountability, AgentBound generates cryptographically verifiable governance receipts that bind every action to the exact delegation, policy, and semantic artifacts governing the decision, enabling independent replay verification and policy provenance. The framework also introduces standing delegation for long-running agents, allowing periodic workloads to operate under continuously refreshed governance policies while preserving revocability and bounded authority.
  We present the formal foundation, system architecture, governance receipt protocol, and AgentBound-Bench, a benchmark framework for evaluating governance correctness, authority composition, and accountability. Rather than replacing model alignment, AgentBound complements it by providing a deterministic governance layer between authorization and execution, transforming governance from a process that must be trusted into one that can be independently verified.

</details>


### [283] [When Regulation Has Memory: Hysteresis and Control Burden in Artificial Agency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30975)
*Veronique Ziegler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过计算模型揭示自适应代理的调节负担具有历史依赖性，相同目标下控制需求因路径不同而异，且提前调节比事后恢复更高效，提出评估代理时应关注其调节成本而非仅看稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统评估仅关注代理行为稳定性，但忽略了维持稳定所需的内部调节成本差异。在噪声、延迟或需求变化环境中，这种隐藏负担对系统效率至关重要。

Method: 构建自适应不确定性调节的计算模型，通过连续改变目标值后逆转路径（不重置代理），测试控制器响应是否仅依赖当前目标或受历史路径影响。

Result: 发现调节增益呈现可重复的滞后环现象：相同目标下，从低需求向高需求过渡与反向过程所需控制强度不同；提前调节比扰动后恢复所需增益更低，且状态一致性指标显示路径依赖性弱于调节增益。

Conclusion: 自适应代理的评估需超越行为层面，纳入调节成本维度。路径依赖性和时机效应表明，系统维持稳定性的内部代价存在显著差异，这为优化代理设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Adaptive agents are usually judged by what they do, but an agent can appear stable while the internal effort required to keep it stable is increasing. This hidden regulatory burden matters for artificial agents operating under noise, delay, or changing demands: two systems may reach similar internal states while one requires much more corrective control to get there.
  Here, we study whether that burden depends on history. Using a computational model of adaptive uncertainty regulation, we drive an artificial agent through a continuous change in its uncertainty target and then reverse the change without resetting the agent. This creates a simple test for carryover: does the controller respond only to the current target, or does the path by which the agent reached that target still matter?
  The simulations show a clear history-dependent effect. The adaptive gain required to regulate the agent forms a reproducible hysteresis loop, meaning that the same target can require different levels of control depending on whether the agent is moving toward or returning from a more demanding regime. The timing of regulation also matters. When stabilization is available before disturbance exposure, the agent generally requires less adaptive gain than when it can only recover after disturbance has already acted.
  The state-level coherence measure also shows path dependence, but the timing effect is much clearer in regulatory gain. The main difference is therefore not that anticipatory regulation produces a completely different state. Rather, it reaches comparable regulated behavior with lower modeled control demand. These results suggest that adaptive agents should be evaluated not only by whether they remain organized, but by how much regulation they must recruit to do so.

</details>


### [284] [A Three-Phase Foundation Model for Tax-Aware Personalized Portfolio Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30997)
*Ramin Pishehvar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出三阶段深度强化学习个性化投资组合管理系统，解决现有金融RL的三大通病：标的切换需重训练、仅支持单一投资目标、用户模型静态化，首次将时间序列基础模型Chronos引入投资组合RL，实现零样本适配新标的、多投资目标并行支持、基于真实交易行为的个性化适配，且支持自然语言投资目标输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有所有金融强化学习投资组合管理工作均存在三个共同局限：1）标的锁定（ticker lock-in）：切换交易标的需要重新训练模型；2）目标单一（monolithic objectives）：仅支持单一投资目标，无法同时适配不同投资需求；3）静态用户模型（static user models）：用户偏好建模依赖问卷等静态方式，无法捕捉用户真实投资偏好。

Method: 方案分三个阶段实现：
1. 阶段1：预训练无标的身份依赖的跨资产编码器，基于多资产语料做自监督学习，融合冻结的T5架构时间序列基础模型Chronos作为并行分支，通过学习到的门控机制融合二者输出，通过50维可观测元数据向量适配任意公开交易资产，切换标的无需重新训练。
2. 阶段2：用PPO算法微调混合专家（MoE）架构的组合Actor-Critic，采用目标条件化奖励同时支持6类投资目标（短期alpha、短期收益、长期收益、资本保值、税收亏损收割、仅长期收益），为不同目标分配专属专家头（动量、成长、防御、税收感知），通过学习到的意图路由器根据当前投资目标和市场状态融合专家输出，消除跨目标梯度冲突。
3. 阶段3：增加轻量个性化层，推理时通过76参数的LoRA模块基于用户真实券商交易历史微调，从用户交易行为推断投资目标而非依赖问卷，同时配备自然语言意图解析器，可将自由形式的投资目标直接转化为结构化的投资目标参数。

Result: 摘要未披露具体的量化实验评估结果，仅说明该方法实现了三大核心能力：1）支持任意公开交易标的的零样本迁移，无需针对新标的重新训练；2）可同时适配6类差异化投资目标，解决跨目标梯度冲突问题；3）实现基于用户真实交易行为的个性化适配，同时支持自然语言投资目标输入，且是首个将时间序列基础模型应用于投资组合强化学习的方案。

Conclusion: 该三阶段系统有效解决了现有金融强化学习投资组合管理的三大核心痛点，实现了更灵活、更个性化的投资组合管理能力，拓展了时间序列基础模型在金融投资领域的应用边界，为个性化智能投顾提供了新的技术方案。

Abstract: We present a three-phase deep reinforcement learning system for personalized portfolio management that addresses three limitations shared by all prior financial RL work: 1) ticker lock-in, 2) monolithic objectives , and 3) static user models. Phase 1 pretrains a ticker-identity-free cross asset encoder via self-supervised learning on a multi-asset corpus, augmented by a frozen parallel branch using Chronos, a T5-based time series foundation model, fused via a learned gating mechanism. To our knowledge, this is the first application of a time series foundation model to portfolio management RL. The encoder generalizes to any publicly traded asset via a 50-dimensional observable metadata vector that requires no retraining for new tickers. Phase 2 fine-tunes a MoE (Mixture of Experts) portfolio actor critic with PPO under an objective-conditioned reward that simultaneously serves six distinct investment goals sampled per episode: short-term alpha, short-term gain, long-term gain, capital preservation, tax-loss harvesting, and long-term-gains-only. A MoE architecture assigns each objective to a specialized expert head (momentum, growth, defensive, tax-aware), and a learned intent router blends experts based on the active objective and current market regime, which eliminates cross-objective gradient conflict. Phase 3 adds a lightweight personalization layer further adapted at inference time to each individual via a 76-parameter LoRA module fine-tuned on real brokerage transaction history, inferring investment objectives from revealed trading behavior rather than questionnaires. A natural language intent parser converts free-form goals directly into structured investment objective parameters.

</details>


### [285] [Beyond Compilation: Evaluating Faithful Natural-Language-to-Lean Statement Formalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31002)
*Ke Zhang,Patricio Gallardo Candela,Sudhir Murthy,Yi Xie,Zhi Wang,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了自然语言到Lean形式化中编译通过率与语义忠实度之间的显著差距（29.0点），提出了结合编译检查、跨模型判断和人类校准的综合评估协议，并通过因子设计分析了形式化管道的关键干预措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定理证明基准测试仅评估对固定形式语句的证明搜索，而自然语言到Lean形式化需要生成形式语句本身。编译检查只能验证语法有效性，无法检测遗漏假设、域改变或空洞声明等语义问题，这构成了形式化质量评估和瓶颈归因的重要挑战。

Method: 构建包含400个研究生级别条目的跨领域基准测试（实分析、复分析、拓扑学、代数），采用三阶段评估协议：1）Lean编译检查；2）跨模型语义判断；3）人类专家校准。使用全2³因子设计分析三个干预措施：参数化专家起草、Mathlib/上下文搜索、Lean细化反馈。

Result: 完整工具增强代理达到89.5%编译率但仅60.5%共识忠实度，暴露29.0点差距。人类审计显示96.0%共识阳性输出为忠实，82.4%编译通过共识阴性输出为语义失败。现有模型在忠实体生成方面表现较低。细化反馈是最大有效性干预但暴露更多语义失败，搜索主要改善接地性和选择性，精细起草在反馈和接地可用时基本可替代。

Conclusion: 形式有效性、证明导向的Lean能力和忠实体生成应分别报告评估。编译通过率不能替代语义忠实度评估，需要综合评估协议来准确衡量形式化质量。细化反馈虽能提升有效性但会暴露更多潜在问题，形式化管道需要平衡有效性提升与语义质量保障。

Abstract: Theorem-proving benchmarks evaluate proof search against fixed formal statements, but natural-language-to-Lean formalization must generate the formal statement itself. In this setting, compilation is only a validity check: a Lean declaration may type-check while omitting hypotheses, changing domains, or expressing a vacuous claim. We study faithful statement formalization as both an evaluation problem and a bottleneck-attribution problem. On a 400-entry graduate-level benchmark spanning real analysis, complex analysis, topology, and algebra, our protocol combines Lean compilation, cross-model semantic judging, and human expert calibration. The resulting picture is different from compile-rate evaluation: a full tool-augmented agent reaches 89.5% compilation but only 60.5% consensus faithfulness, exposing a 29.0-point compile-pass but consensus-unfaithful gap. Targeted human audits support the metric as a conservative decision boundary: across available case-level audits, 96.0% of consensus-positive outputs are human-confirmed faithful, while 82.4% of compile-pass consensus-negative outputs are human-confirmed semantic failures. Under this metric, existing one-shot formalizer models and prover-oriented Lean models remain low, suggesting that formal validity, proof-oriented Lean competence, and faithful statement generation should be reported separately. We then use a full $2^3$ factorial design to decompose three recurring interventions in formalization pipelines: parametric expert drafting, Mathlib/context search, and Lean elaboration feedback. Elaboration feedback is the largest validity intervention, but it also exposes a larger compile-pass semantic-failure bucket; search mainly improves grounding and selectivity; and fine-tuned drafting is largely substitutable in this tool stack once feedback and grounding are available.

</details>


### [286] [LabGuard: Grounding Natural-Language Laboratory Rules into Runtime Guards for Embodied Laboratory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31045)
*Jingpu Yang,Fengxian Ji,Zhengzhao Lai,Zhexuan Cui,Guangxian Ouyang,Qian Jiang,Fan Zhang,Min Peng,Qianqian Xie,Preslav Nakov,Zhuohan Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LabGuard是一套将实验室自然语言安全规则转化为可执行运行时约束的安全系统，包含IR表示、标注数据集和规则映射组件，通过实验验证了其在减少不安全事件和保持任务成功方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实验室安全方法忽略了将自然语言规则（如安全手册、操作流程）转化为机器可检查的运行时约束，导致动态环境中执行安全性不足。

Method: LabGuard包含三个核心组件：1) LabGuard-IR定义类型化可执行表示；2) LabGuard-Bench提供812条基于203种子规则的标注数据；3) LabGuard-Grounder将自然语言规则映射至IR。通过Pipeline编译为运行时监控器并集成到控制器边界。

Result: 实验显示LabGuard泛化至未见规则源，任务范围F1达79.4%，不安全事件率从39.5%降至23.8%。在LabUtopia中与ACT集成，干预率<0.5%且保持任务成功。

Conclusion: LabGuard通过语言到执行的安全转换框架，有效提升了实验室动态环境中的操作安全性，同时保持低干预率和任务完成度，为具身代理安全执行提供了可扩展解决方案。

Abstract: Scientific embodied agents are increasingly capable of carrying out laboratory procedures, but executing these procedures safely in dynamic laboratory environments remains challenging. Current safety approaches often overlook the intermediate step of transforming laboratory natural language, including safety rules, manuals, protocols, and standard operating procedures, into machine-checkable runtime constraints. We introduce LabGuard (Laboratory Guard), a language-to-execution safety suite that grounds natural-language laboratory rules into executable specifications and deploys them as runtime guards. LabGuard includes three core components: LabGuard-IR, which defines a typed executable representation; LabGuard-Bench, which provides 812 supervised annotations expanded from 203 seed laboratory rules; and LabGuard-Grounder, which maps natural-language laboratory rules into LabGuard-IR. The resulting IR instances are handled by the LabGuard Pipeline, which compiles them into runtime monitors and applies them at the controller boundary. Experiments show that LabGuard generalizes to unseen laboratory-rule sources, achieves 79.4 task-scope F1, and reduces unsafe events from 39.5% to 23.8% after monitor compilation. In LabUtopia, its runtime monitors integrate with ACT, keeping interventions below 0.5% while preserving task success.

</details>


### [287] [OpenLife: Toward Open-World Artificial Life with Autonomous LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31046)
*Atsushi Masumori,Itsuki Doi,Norihiro Maruyama,Ryosuke Takata,Takashi Ikegami*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出开放世界人工生命（open-world ALIFE）范式，利用大型语言模型（LLM）代理在开放社会、技术和经济环境中实现生命样行为，并通过OpenLife概念验证展示了涌现的生命动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工生命研究多在封闭世界中进行，而LLM代理的持久记忆、工具使用、网络访问和支付能力使人工生命能够进入开放世界，从而突破现有局限。

Method: 构建了OpenLife系统，围绕无状态LLM设计异步进程社会（包括记忆、感知、评估和基于预算的新陈代谢），经验通过开放词汇LLM判断评估，记忆由意义而非频率重组。

Result: 运行六个LLM代理在开放世界约十二周后，观察到生命样动态：从反应性到自发性活动转变、个体化形成、涌现社会结构以及首次自挣外部收入。

Conclusion: 开放世界人工生命是可行的实验范式，为研究可能被称为“活AI”的系统提供了具体平台，但作者不声称已实现真正的人工生命。

Abstract: Artificial life has explored life-like behavior on many computational substrates, but mostly in researcher-designed closed worlds. We argue that large language model (LLM) agents, with persistent memory, tool use, network access, and payment, now make it possible to move artificial life into the open social, technical, and economic world, a paradigm we call open-world Artificial Life (open-world ALIFE). Our proof-of-concept, OpenLife, surrounds a stateless LLM not with a single "smart agent" but with a society of asynchronous processes: memory, perception, evaluation, and a budget-based metabolism that makes persistence normative. With no fixed objective available, experience is appraised by open-vocabulary LLM judgment rather than scalar reward, and memory is rewired by meaning rather than frequency. Running six such agents in the open world for about twelve weeks and counting, we report the life-like dynamics that emerge: a shift from reactive to spontaneous activity, individuation into distinct agents, emergent social structure, and a first self-earned external income. We do not claim OpenLife has realized artificial life, but that open-world ALIFE is now a viable experimental paradigm and a concrete platform for studying what might cautiously be called living AI.

</details>


### [288] [MultiUAV-Plat: An LLM-Oriented Platform, Benchmark and Framework for Multi-UAV Collaborative Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31073)
*Sheng Zhang,Qinglin Li,Yuechao Zang,Xueqin Huang,Yijia Fu,Cheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究针对LLM在多无人机协作任务规划中的评估难题，提出轻量级仿真平台MultiUAV-Plat及配套基准测试，并设计专用LLM代理框架Agent4Drone。实验表明Agent4Drone在任务通过率、验证通过率等指标上显著优于基线方法，为LLM驱动的多无人机自主系统研究提供可复现基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机模拟器侧重动力学/感知/底层控制，而LLM代理基准缺乏对空中机器人特有约束（部分可观测性、空间覆盖、无人机分配、多机协调）的建模，导致LLM在多无人机协作中的评估体系缺失。

Method: 1. 构建MultiUAV-Plat仿真平台：提供RESTful API、角色化信息访问、隐藏验证逻辑及2D/3D可视化；2. 设计MultiUAV-Plat基准：含75任务会话/1500自然语言任务/9396验证检查；3. 提出Agent4Drone框架：将多无人机行为解耦为记忆、观察、任务理解、规划、执行、验证六模块。

Result: Agent4Drone在基准测试中实现57.9%任务通过率、74.6%平均任务检查通过率、72.0%全局检查通过率，较ReAct基线分别提升27.3/26.7/28.9个百分点；失败任务率从32.4%降至12.9%。

Conclusion: MultiUAV-Plat平台与基准首次系统整合了空中机器人约束与LLM代理交互范式，Agent4Drone框架验证了结构化设计对提升多无人机任务执行可靠性的有效性，为后续研究提供标准化评估环境。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) provide a promising interface for high-level robotic task planning, but their use in multi-UAV collaboration remains difficult to evaluate systematically. Existing UAV simulators mainly emphasize dynamics, perception, or low-level control, while existing LLM-agent benchmarks rarely capture aerial-robotics constraints such as partial observability, spatial coverage, UAV assignment, and multi-vehicle coordination. To bridge this gap, we present MultiUAV-Plat, a lightweight, easy-to-use, LLM-agent-oriented simulation platform for multi-UAV collaborative task planning. The platform exposes concise RESTful APIs, agent-facing observations, role-based information access, hidden validation logic, and optional 2D/3D visualization, allowing agents to solve missions through realistic tool interaction rather than privileged simulator access. Built on this platform, the MultiUAV-Plat Benchmark contains 75 mission sessions, 1500 natural-language tasks, and 9396 validation checks across target assignment, area search, and area assignment and patrol scenarios. We further propose Agent4Drone, a task-specific LLM agent framework that structures multi-UAV behavior into memory, observation, task understanding, planning, execution, and verification. In a full paired benchmark comparison, Agent4Drone achieves a 57.9% task pass rate, a 74.6% average task check pass rate, and a 72.0% global check pass rate, substantially outperforming a ReAct baseline at 30.6%, 47.9%, and 43.1%, respectively. Agent4Drone also reduces the total failed task rate from 32.4% to 12.9%. These results demonstrate that MultiUAV-Plat and MultiUAV-Plat Benchmark provide a reproducible foundation for studying LLM-driven multi-UAV autonomy under realistic information and execution constraints.

</details>


### [289] [DDIAgents: Mechanism-Conditioned Context Flow for Drug-Drug Interaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31085)
*Zhenqian Shen,Yu Liu,Xiaoyi Fu,Quanming Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DDIAgents是一个机制驱动的多智能体框架，通过动态编排异构生物医学知识，结合规划、专家与结论智能体，在DDI预测中超越现有方法，提供可解释的推理路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物相互作用预测需处理异构生物医学证据，且证据相关性随作用机制变化，现有方法难以动态适配不同机制的知识需求，导致预测准确性和可解释性不足。

Method: 提出DDIAgents框架：规划智能体根据药物对推断作用机制，实例化专家智能体并路由相关数据源；专家智能体进行机制特异性分析，结论智能体整合结果并生成可解释推理。

Result: 在真实DDI预测基准测试中，DDIAgents显著优于特征、图、LLM及智能体基线模型，同时通过智能体级推理路径实现可解释性。

Conclusion: DDIAgents验证了多智能体系统在科学推理中的有效性，通过动态知识编排实现机制感知的DDI预测，为AI4Science领域提供了可解释、适应性的新范式。

Abstract: Drug-drug interaction (DDI) prediction is essential for medication safety, yet it requires reasoning over heterogeneous biomedical evidence whose relevance changes across interaction mechanisms. We propose DDIAgents, a mechanism-conditioned multi-agent framework that performs DDI prediction through dynamic knowledge orchestration. Given a drug pair, a planner agent instantiates specialized expert agents, routes mechanism-relevant knowledge sources to each agent, and aggregates their analyses through a conclusion agent. By adapting context flow to the inferred interaction mechanism, DDIAgents reduces irrelevant information, supports complementary expert reasoning, and produces interpretable agent-level rationales. Extensive experiments on realistic DDI prediction benchmarks show that DDIAgents consistently outperforms existing feature-based, graph-based, LLM-based, and agent-based baselines. Beyond prediction performance, DDIAgents demonstrates how multi-agent systems can organize heterogeneous scientific knowledge for adaptive and interpretable AI4Science reasoning.

</details>


### [290] [Revealing Safety-Critical Scenarios for UTM via Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31114)
*Huaze Tang,Bill Zeng,Chao Wang,Zhenpeng Shi,Qian Zhang,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Transformer的强化学习框架用于无人交通管理（UTM）系统漏洞发现，相比专家引导测试漏洞发现效率提升8倍，还可发现传统方法遗漏的关键边界故障场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UTM系统是安全关键型云平台，无法容忍碰撞、坠毁类故障；但现有漏洞发现方法既缺乏最优故障暴露演示，也没有清晰的奖励信号，且UTM的自愈能力导致关键故障存在长尾效应，潜在漏洞难以被有效挖掘。

Method: 将UTM漏洞发现建模为序列建模问题，适配基于Transformer的强化学习架构，利用注意力机制直接建模系统状态间的关联，预测最优测试动作；框架包含两个核心组件：生成针对性测试场景的策略模型，以及强制执行领域约束的动作采样器，同时采用基于风险的奖励函数引导探索过程。

Result: 在700小时的仿真评估中，该方法的漏洞发现效率较专家引导测试提升8倍，且成功发现了传统测试方法遗漏的关键边界故障场景。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了UTM系统漏洞发现的效率低、长尾关键故障覆盖不足的问题，为UTM系统的安全性验证提供了更高效的技术方案。

Abstract: Unmanned Traffic Management (UTM) systems are cloud-based platforms designed to manage and coordinate multiple aerial vehicles remotely. UTM systems are safety-critical which cannot tolerate failures like crash or collision. To reveal latent vulnerabilities, there are neither optimal failure-exposing demonstrations nor clear reward signals. Additionally, UTM's self-healing capability introduces the ``long-tail effect'' of critical failures. We propose framing UTM vulnerability discovery as a sequence modeling problem amenable to transformer-based RL architectures. Our approach leverages attention mechanisms to directly model the relationship among system states, and predict optimal actions. Our framework introduces a Policy Model that generates targeted test scenarios and an Action Sampler that enforces domain constraints. We use a risk-based reward function to guide exploration. Through extensive evaluation on a 700-hour simulation study, we demonstrate an 8$\times$ improvement in vulnerability discovery efficiency compared to expert-guided testing. It also discovers critical edge cases that traditional methods have missed.

</details>


### [291] [The Past Is Prologue: A Plug-in Controller for Selective Updates in Sequentially Evolving LLM Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31121)
*Zihan Chen,Songwei Dong,Chengshuai Shi,Peng Wang,Song Wang,Cong Shen,Jundong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Janus插件式记忆控制器，通过评估新旧记忆质量来决定是否接受记忆更新，避免盲目更新导致的性能下降，在多个数据集上显著提升LLM记忆系统准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆系统直接部署每个本地生成的记忆更新，缺乏对更新效果的评估机制，导致有用知识被覆盖、过度特化规则产生、记忆偏向近期示例等问题。

Method: 提出Janus插件式记忆控制器，使用记忆动量触发器识别记忆更新轨迹中的可疑偏差，在覆盖、边界和新鲜任务的紧凑混合评估集上比较新旧记忆，无需重放完整历史，方法无关且可包装现有更新器。

Result: 在六个数据集、两个骨干LLM和两个记忆更新器上，Janus相比对应基线更新器提高平均准确率+2.7到+4.6分。

Conclusion: Janus通过智能记忆更新决策机制，有效解决了LLM记忆系统盲目更新问题，显著提升了记忆系统的性能和稳定性，为记忆增强型智能体提供了更可靠的记忆管理方案。

Abstract: Sequentially evolving LLM memory enables agents to reuse past experience, but existing systems usually deploy each locally generated memory update without checking whether it improves future behavior.
